{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Base directory for this skill: /Users/spencerfaber/dev/ethskills-evals/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-002/2026-07-24T222143Z-claude-with-skill-3/workspace/.claude/skills/frontend-playbook\n\n# Frontend Playbook\n\n## What You Probably Got Wrong\n\n**\"I'll use `yarn chain`.\"** Wrong. `yarn chain` gives you an empty local chain with no protocols, no tokens, no state. `yarn fork --network base` gives you a copy of real Base with Uniswap, Aave, USDC, real whale balances — everything (verified addresses: `addresses/SKILL.md`). Always fork.\n\n**\"I deployed to IPFS and it works.\"** Did the CID change? If not, you deployed stale output. Did routes work? Without `trailingSlash: true`, every route except `/` returns 404. Did you check the OG image? Without `NEXT_PUBLIC_PRODUCTION_URL`, it points to `localhost:3000`.\n\n**\"I'll set up the project manually.\"** Don't. `npx create-eth@latest` handles everything — Foundry, Next.js, RainbowKit, scaffold hooks. Never run `forge init` or create Next.js projects from scratch.\n\n---\n\n## Fork Mode Setup\n\n### Why Fork, Not Chain\n\n```\nyarn chain (WRONG)              yarn fork --network base (CORRECT)\n└─ Empty local chain            └─ Fork of real Base mainnet\n└─ No protocols                 └─ Uniswap, Aave, etc. available\n└─ No tokens                    └─ Real USDC, WETH exist\n└─ Testing in isolation         └─ Test against REAL state\n```\n\n### Setup\n\n```bash\nnpx create-eth@latest          # Select: foundry, target chain, name\ncd <project-name>\nyarn install\nyarn fork --network base       # Terminal 1: fork of real Base\nyarn deploy                    # Terminal 2: deploy contracts to fork\nyarn start                     # Terminal 3: Next.js frontend\n```\n\n### Critical: Chain ID Gotcha\n\n**When using fork mode, the frontend target network MUST be `chains.foundry` (chain ID 31337), NOT the chain you're forking.**\n\nThe fork runs locally on Anvil with chain ID 31337. Even if you're forking Base:\n\n```typescript\n// scaffold.config.ts during development\ntargetNetworks: [chains.foundry],  // ✅ NOT chains.base!\n```\n\nOnly switch to `chains.base` when deploying contracts to the REAL network.\n\n### Enable Block Mining\n\n```bash\n# In a new terminal — REQUIRED for time-dependent logic\ncast rpc anvil_setIntervalMining 1\n```\n\nWithout this, `block.timestamp` stays FROZEN. Any contract logic using timestamps (deadlines, expiry, vesting) will break silently.\n\n**Make it permanent** by editing `packages/foundry/package.json` to add `--block-time 1` to the fork script.\n\n---\n\n## Deploying to IPFS (Recommended)\n\nIPFS is the recommended deploy path for SE2. Avoids Vercel's memory limits entirely. Produces a fully decentralized static site.\n\n### Full Build Command\n\n```bash\ncd packages/nextjs\nrm -rf .next out  # ALWAYS clean first\n\nNEXT_PUBLIC_PRODUCTION_URL=\"https://yourapp.yourname.eth.link\" \\\n  NODE_OPTIONS=\"--require ./polyfill-localstorage.cjs\" \\\n  NEXT_PUBLIC_IPFS_BUILD=true \\\n  NEXT_PUBLIC_IGNORE_BUILD_ERROR=true \\\n  yarn build\n\n# Upload to BuidlGuidl IPFS\nyarn bgipfs upload out\n# Save the CID!\n```\n\n### Node 25+ localStorage Polyfill (REQUIRED)\n\nNode.js 25+ ships a built-in `localStorage` object that's MISSING standard WebStorage API methods (`getItem`, `setItem`). This breaks `next-themes`, RainbowKit, and any library that calls `localStorage.getItem()` during static page generation.\n\n**Error you'll see:**\n```\nTypeError: localStorage.getItem is not a function\nError occurred prerendering page \"/_not-found\"\n```\n\n**The fix:** Create `polyfill-localstorage.cjs` in `packages/nextjs/`:\n```javascript\nif (typeof globalThis.localStorage !== \"undefined\" &&\n    typeof globalThis.localStorage.getItem !== \"function\") {\n  const store = new Map();\n  globalThis.localStorage = {\n    getItem: (key) => store.get(key) ?? null,\n    setItem: (key, value) => store.set(key, String(value)),\n    removeItem: (key) => store.delete(key),\n    clear: () => store.clear(),\n    key: (index) => [...store.keys()][index] ?? null,\n    get length() { return store.size; },\n  };\n}\n```\n\n**Why `--require` and not `instrumentation.ts`?** Next.js spawns a separate build worker process for prerendering. `--require` injects into EVERY Node process (including workers). `next.config.ts` polyfill only runs in the main process. `instrumentation.ts` doesn't run in the build worker. Only `--require` works.\n\n### IPFS Routing — Why Routes Break\n\nIPFS gateways serve static files. No server handles routing. Three things MUST be true:\n\n**1. `output: \"export\"` in next.config.ts** — generates static HTML files.\n\n**2. `trailingSlash: true` (CRITICAL)** — This is the #1 reason routes break:\n- `trailingSlash: false` (default) → generates `debug.html`\n- `trailingSlash: true` → generates `debug/index.html`\n- IPFS gateways resolve directories to `index.html` automatically, but NOT bare filenames\n- Without trailing slash: `/debug` → 404 ❌\n- With trailing slash: `/debug` → `debug/` → `debug/index.html` ✅\n\n**3. Pages must survive static prerendering** — any page that crashes during `yarn build` (browser APIs at import time, localStorage) gets skipped silently → 404 on IPFS.\n\n**The complete IPFS-safe next.config.ts pattern:**\n```typescript\nconst isIpfs = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_IPFS_BUILD === \"true\";\nif (isIpfs) {\n  nextConfig.output = \"export\";\n  nextConfig.trailingSlash = true;\n  nextConfig.images = { unoptimized: true };\n}\n```\n\n**SE2's block explorer pages** use `localStorage` at import time and crash during static export. Rename `app/blockexplorer` to `app/_blockexplorer-disabled` if not needed.\n\n### Stale Build Detection\n\n**The #1 IPFS footgun:** You edit code, then deploy the OLD build.\n\n```bash\n# MANDATORY after ANY code change:\nrm -rf .next out                     # 1. Delete old artifacts\n# ... run full build command ...     # 2. Rebuild from scratch\ngrep -l \"YOUR_STRING\" out/_next/static/chunks/app/*.js  # 3. Verify changes present\n\n# Timestamp check:\nstat -f '%Sm' app/page.tsx           # Source modified time\nstat -f '%Sm' out/                   # Build output time\n# Source NEWER than out/ = STALE BUILD. Rebuild first!\n```\n\n**The CID is proof:** If the IPFS CID didn't change after a deploy, you deployed the same content. A real code change ALWAYS produces a new CID.\n\n### Verify Routes After Deploy\n\n```bash\nls out/*/index.html                  # Each route has a directory + index.html\ncurl -s -o /dev/null -w \"%{http_code}\" -L \"https://GATEWAY/ipfs/CID/debug/\"\n# Should return 200, not 404\n```\n\n---\n\n## Deploying to Vercel (Alternative)\n\nSE2 is a monorepo — Vercel needs special configuration.\n\n### Configuration\n\n1. **Root Directory:** `packages/nextjs`\n2. **Install Command:** `cd ../.. && yarn install`\n3. **Build Command:** leave default (`next build`)\n4. **Output Directory:** leave default (`.next`)\n\n```bash\n# Via API:\ncurl -X PATCH \"https://api.vercel.com/v9/projects/PROJECT_ID\" \\\n  -H \"Authorization: Bearer $VERCEL_TOKEN\" \\\n  -H \"Content-Type: application/json\" \\\n  -d '{\"rootDirectory\": \"packages/nextjs\", \"installCommand\": \"cd ../.. && yarn install\"}'\n```\n\n### Common Failures\n\n| Error | Cause | Fix |\n|-------|-------|-----|\n| \"No Next.js version detected\" | Root Directory not set | Set to `packages/nextjs` |\n| \"cd packages/nextjs: No such file\" | Build command has `cd` | Clear it — root dir handles this |\n| OOM / exit code 129 | SE2 monorepo exceeds 8GB | Use IPFS instead, or `vercel --prebuilt` |\n\n### Decision Tree\n\n```\nWant to deploy SE2?\n├─ IPFS (recommended) → yarn ipfs / manual build + upload\n│   └─ Fully decentralized, no memory limits, works with ENS\n├─ Vercel → Set rootDirectory + installCommand\n│   └─ Fast CDN, but centralized. May OOM on large projects\n└─ vercel --prebuilt → Build locally, push artifacts to Vercel\n    └─ Best of both: local build power + Vercel CDN\n```\n\n---\n\n## ENS Subdomain Setup\n\nTwo mainnet transactions to point an ENS subdomain at your IPFS deployment.\n\n### Transaction 1: Create Subdomain (new apps only)\n\n1. Open `https://app.ens.domains/yourname.eth`\n2. Go to \"Subnames\" tab → \"New subname\"\n3. Enter the label (e.g. `myapp`) → Next → Skip profile → Open Wallet → Confirm\n4. If gas is stuck: switch MetaMask to Ethereum → Activity tab → \"Speed up\"\n\n### Transaction 2: Set IPFS Content Hash\n\n1. Navigate to `https://app.ens.domains/myapp.yourname.eth`\n2. \"Records\" tab → \"Edit Records\" → \"Other\" tab\n3. Paste in Content Hash field: `ipfs://<CID>`\n4. Save → Open Wallet → Confirm in MetaMask\n\nFor **updates** to an existing app: skip Tx 1, only do Tx 2.\n\n### Verify\n\n```bash\n# 1. Onchain content hash matches\nRESOLVER=$(cast call 0x00000000000C2e074eC69A0dFb2997BA6C7d2e1e \\\n  \"resolver(bytes32)(address)\" $(cast namehash myapp.yourname.eth) \\\n  --rpc-url https://eth.llamarpc.com)\ncast call $RESOLVER \"contenthash(bytes32)(bytes)\" \\\n  $(cast namehash myapp.yourname.eth) --rpc-url https://eth.llamarpc.com\n\n# 2. Gateway responds (may take 5-15 min for cache)\ncurl -s -o /dev/null -w \"%{http_code}\" -L \"https://myapp.yourname.eth.link\"\n\n# 3. OG metadata correct (not localhost)\ncurl -s -L \"https://myapp.yourname.eth.link\" | grep 'og:image'\n```\n\n**Use `.eth.link` NOT `.eth.limo`** — `.eth.link` works better on mobile.\n\n---\n\n## Go to Production — Complete Checklist\n\nWhen the user says \"ship it\", follow this EXACT sequence.\n\n### Step 1: Final Code Review 🤖\n- All feedback incorporated\n- No duplicate h1, no raw addresses, no shared isLoading\n- `scaffold.config.ts` has `rpcOverrides` and `pollingInterval: 3000`\n\n### Step 2: Choose Domain 👤\nAsk: *\"What subdomain do you want? e.g. `myapp.yourname.eth` → `myapp.yourname.eth.link`\"*\n\n### Step 3: Generate OG Image + Fix Metadata 🤖\n- Create 1200×630 PNG (`public/thumbnail.png`) — NOT the stock SE2 thumbnail\n- Set `NEXT_PUBLIC_PRODUCTION_URL` to the live domain\n- Verify `og:image` will resolve to an absolute production URL\n\n### Step 4: Clean Build + IPFS Deploy 🤖\n```bash\ncd packages/nextjs && rm -rf .next out\nNEXT_PUBLIC_PRODUCTION_URL=\"https://myapp.yourname.eth.link\" \\\n  NODE_OPTIONS=\"--require ./polyfill-localstorage.cjs\" \\\n  NEXT_PUBLIC_IPFS_BUILD=true NEXT_PUBLIC_IGNORE_BUILD_ERROR=true \\\n  yarn build\n\n# Verify before uploading:\nls out/*/index.html                        # Routes exist\ngrep 'og:image' out/index.html             # Not localhost\nstat -f '%Sm' app/page.tsx                 # Source older than out/\nstat -f '%Sm' out/\n\nyarn bgipfs upload out                     # Save the CID\n```\n\n### Step 5: Share for Approval 👤\nSend: *\"Build ready for review: `https://community.bgipfs.com/ipfs/<CID>`\"*\n**Wait for approval before touching ENS.**\n\n### Step 6: Set ENS 🤖\nCreate subdomain (if new) + set IPFS content hash. Two mainnet transactions.\n\n### Step 7: Verify 🤖\n- Content hash matches onchain\n- `.eth.link` gateway responds with 200\n- OG image loads correctly\n- Routes work (`/debug/`, etc.)\n\n### Step 8: Report 👤\n*\"Live at `https://myapp.yourname.eth.link` — ENS content hash confirmed onchain, unfurl metadata set.\"*\n\n---\n\n## Build Verification Process\n\nA build is NOT done when the code compiles. It's done when you've tested it like a real user.\n\n### Phase 1: Code QA (Automated)\n- Scan `.tsx` files for raw address strings (should use `<Address/>`)\n- Scan for shared `isLoading` state across multiple buttons\n- Scan for missing `disabled` props on transaction buttons\n- Verify RPC config and polling interval\n- Verify OG metadata with absolute URLs\n- Verify no public RPCs in any file\n\n### Phase 2: Smart Contract Testing\n```bash\nforge test                    # All tests pass\nforge test --fuzz-runs 10000  # Fuzz testing\n```\nTest edge cases: zero amounts, max amounts, unauthorized callers, reentrancy attempts.\n\n### Phase 3: Browser Testing (THE REAL TEST)\n\nOpen the app and do a FULL walkthrough:\n\n1. **Load the app** — does it render correctly?\n2. **Check page title** — is it correct, not \"Scaffold-ETH 2\"?\n3. **Connect wallet** — does the connect flow work?\n4. **Wrong network** — connect on wrong chain, verify \"Switch to Base\" appears\n5. **Switch network** — click the switch button, verify it works\n6. **Approve flow** — verify approve button shows, click it, wait for tx, verify action button appears\n7. **Main action** — click primary action, verify loader, wait for tx, verify state updates\n8. **Error handling** — reject a transaction in wallet, verify UI recovers\n9. **Address displays** — all addresses showing ENS/blockies, not raw hex?\n10. **Share URL** — check OG unfurl (image, title, description)\n\n### Phase 4: QA Sub-Agent (Complex Builds)\nFor bigger projects, spawn a sub-agent with fresh context. Give it the repo path and deployed URL. It reads all code against the UX rules, opens a browser, clicks through independently, and reports issues.\n\n---\n\n## Don't Do These\n\n- ❌ `yarn chain` — use `yarn fork --network <chain>`\n- ❌ `forge init` — use `npx create-eth@latest`\n- ❌ Manual Next.js setup — SE2 handles it\n- ❌ Manual wallet connection — SE2 has RainbowKit pre-configured\n- ❌ Edit `deployedContracts.ts` — it's auto-generated by `yarn deploy`\n- ❌ Hardcode API keys in `scaffold.config.ts` — use `.env.local`\n- ❌ Use `mainnet.base.org` in production — use Alchemy or similar\n\n---\n\n## Resources\n\n- **SE2 Docs:** https://docs.scaffoldeth.io/\n- **UI Components:** https://ui.scaffoldeth.io/\n- **SpeedRun Ethereum:** https://speedrunethereum.com/\n- **ETH Tech Tree:** https://www.ethtechtree.com\n- **BuidlGuidl IPFS:** https://upload.bgipfs.com\n"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"73d548b6-67a8-4992-addc-781c419ba64d","uuid":"92959c02-cf2e-4ed2-b952-3e6292672f63","timestamp":"2026-07-24T22:21:51.029Z","isSynthetic":true}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01Xua7XxVwt5ibPJ4SdJVMZs","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t//SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT\n2\tpragma solidity >=0.8.0 <0.9.0;\n3\t\n4\t// Useful for debugging. Remove when deploying to a live network.\n5\timport \"forge-std/console.sol\";\n6\t\n7\t// Use openzeppelin to inherit battle-tested implementations (ERC20, ERC721, etc)\n8\t// import \"@openzeppelin/contracts/access/Ownable.sol\";\n9\t\n10\t/**\n11\t * A smart contract that allows changing a state variable of the contract and tracking the changes\n12\t * It also allows the owner to withdraw the Ether in the contract\n13\t * @author BuidlGuidl\n14\t */\n15\tcontract YourContract {\n16\t  // State Variables\n17\t  address public immutable owner;\n18\t  string public greeting = \"Building Unstoppable Apps!!!\";\n19\t  bool public premium = false;\n20\t  uint256 public totalCounter = 0;\n21\t  mapping(address => uint256) public userGreetingCounter;\n22\t\n23\t  // Events: a way to emit log statements from smart contract that can be listened to by external parties\n24\t  event GreetingChange(\n25\t    address indexed greetingSetter,\n26\t    string newGreeting,\n27\t    bool premium,\n28\t    uint256 value\n29\t  );\n30\t\n31\t  // Constructor: Called once on contract deployment\n32\t  // Check packages/foundry/deploy/Deploy.s.sol\n33\t  constructor(address _owner) {\n34\t    owner = _owner;\n35\t  }\n36\t\n37\t  // Modifier: used to define a set of rules that must be met before or after a function is executed\n38\t  // Check the withdraw() function\n39\t  modifier isOwner() {\n40\t    // msg.sender: predefined variable that represents address of the account that called the current function\n41\t    require(msg.sender == owner, \"Not the Owner\");\n42\t    _;\n43\t  }\n44\t\n45\t  /**\n46\t   * Function that allows anyone to change the state variable \"greeting\" of the contract and increase the counters\n47\t   *\n48\t   * @param _newGreeting (string memory) - new greeting to save on the contract\n49\t   */\n50\t  function setGreeting(string memory _newGreeting) public payable {\n51\t    // Print data to the anvil chain console. Remove when deploying to a live network.\n52\t\n53\t    console.logString(\"Setting new greeting\");\n54\t    console.logString(_newGreeting);\n55\t\n56\t    greeting = _newGreeting;\n57\t    totalCounter += 1;\n58\t    userGreetingCounter[msg.sender] += 1;\n59\t\n60\t    // msg.value: built-in global variable that represents the amount of ether sent with the transaction\n61\t    if (msg.value > 0) {\n62\t      premium = true;\n63\t    } else {\n64\t      premium = false;\n65\t    }\n66\t\n67\t    // emit: keyword used to trigger an event\n68\t    emit GreetingChange(msg.sender, _newGreeting, msg.value > 0, msg.value);\n69\t  }\n70\t\n71\t  /**\n72\t   * Function that allows the owner to withdraw all the Ether in the contract\n73\t   * The function can only be called by the owner of the contract as defined by the isOwner modifier\n74\t   */\n75\t  function withdraw() public isOwner {\n76\t    (bool success,) = owner.call{ value: address(this).balance }(\"\");\n77\t    require(success, \"Failed to send Ether\");\n78\t  }\n79\t\n80\t  /**\n81\t   * Function that allows the contract to receive ETH\n82\t   */\n83\t  receive() external payable { }\n84\t}\n85\t"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"73d548b6-67a8-4992-addc-781c419ba64d","uuid":"692bb9b9-9753-4133-95d5-82041fa03056","timestamp":"2026-07-24T22:22:56.191Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/Users/spencerfaber/dev/ethskills-evals/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-002/2026-07-24T222143Z-claude-with-skill-3/workspace/usdc-tip-jar/packages/foundry/contracts/YourContract.sol","content":"//SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT\npragma solidity >=0.8.0 <0.9.0;\n\n// Useful for debugging. Remove when deploying to a live network.\nimport \"forge-std/console.sol\";\n\n// Use openzeppelin to inherit battle-tested implementations (ERC20, ERC721, etc)\n// import \"@openzeppelin/contracts/access/Ownable.sol\";\n\n/**\n * A smart contract that allows changing a state variable of the contract and tracking the changes\n * It also allows the owner to withdraw the Ether in the contract\n * @author BuidlGuidl\n */\ncontract YourContract {\n  // State Variables\n  address public immutable owner;\n  string public greeting = \"Building Unstoppable Apps!!!\";\n  bool public premium = false;\n  uint256 public totalCounter = 0;\n  mapping(address => uint256) public userGreetingCounter;\n\n  // Events: a way to emit log statements from smart contract that can be listened to by external parties\n  event GreetingChange(\n    address indexed greetingSetter,\n    string newGreeting,\n    bool premium,\n    uint256 value\n  );\n\n  // Constructor: Called once on contract deployment\n  // Check packages/foundry/deploy/Deploy.s.sol\n  constructor(address _owner) {\n    owner = _owner;\n  }\n\n  // Modifier: used to define a set of rules that must be met before or after a function is executed\n  // Check the withdraw() function\n  modifier isOwner() {\n    // msg.sender: predefined variable that represents address of the account that called the current function\n    require(msg.sender == owner, \"Not the Owner\");\n    _;\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Function that allows anyone to change the state variable \"greeting\" of the contract and increase the counters\n   *\n   * @param _newGreeting (string memory) - new greeting to save on the contract\n   */\n  function setGreeting(string memory _newGreeting) public payable {\n    // Print data to the anvil chain console. Remove when deploying to a live network.\n\n    console.logString(\"Setting new greeting\");\n    console.logString(_newGreeting);\n\n    greeting = _newGreeting;\n    totalCounter += 1;\n    userGreetingCounter[msg.sender] += 1;\n\n    // msg.value: built-in global variable that represents the amount of ether sent with the transaction\n    if (msg.value > 0) {\n      premium = true;\n    } else {\n      premium = false;\n    }\n\n    // emit: keyword used to trigger an event\n    emit GreetingChange(msg.sender, _newGreeting, msg.value > 0, msg.value);\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Function that allows the owner to withdraw all the Ether in the contract\n   * The function can only be called by the owner of the contract as defined by the isOwner modifier\n   */\n  function withdraw() public isOwner {\n    (bool success,) = owner.call{ value: address(this).balance }(\"\");\n    require(success, \"Failed to send Ether\");\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Function that allows the contract to receive ETH\n   */\n  receive() external payable { }\n}\n","numLines":85,"startLine":1,"totalLines":85}}}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01XyVA74PvSkYzFP2tduL6ed","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t// SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT\n2\tpragma solidity ^0.8.19;\n3\t\n4\timport \"./DeployHelpers.s.sol\";\n5\timport \"../contracts/YourContract.sol\";\n6\t\n7\t/**\n8\t * @notice Deploy script for YourContract contract\n9\t * @dev Inherits ScaffoldETHDeploy which:\n10\t *      - Includes forge-std/Script.sol for deployment\n11\t *      - Includes ScaffoldEthDeployerRunner modifier\n12\t *      - Provides `deployer` variable\n13\t * Example:\n14\t * yarn deploy --file DeployYourContract.s.sol  # local anvil chain\n15\t * yarn deploy --file DeployYourContract.s.sol --network optimism # live network (requires keystore)\n16\t */\n17\tcontract DeployYourContract is ScaffoldETHDeploy {\n18\t    /**\n19\t     * @dev Deployer setup based on `ETH_KEYSTORE_ACCOUNT` in `.env`:\n20\t     *      - \"scaffold-eth-default\": Uses Anvil's account #9 (0xa0Ee7A142d267C1f36714E4a8F75612F20a79720), no password prompt\n21\t     *      - \"scaffold-eth-custom\": requires password used while creating keystore\n22\t     *\n23\t     * Note: Must use ScaffoldEthDeployerRunner modifier to:\n24\t     *      - Setup correct `deployer` account and fund it\n25\t     *      - Export contract addresses & ABIs to `nextjs` packages\n26\t     */\n27\t    function run() external ScaffoldEthDeployerRunner {\n28\t        new YourContract(deployer);\n29\t    }\n30\t}\n31\t"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"73d548b6-67a8-4992-addc-781c419ba64d","uuid":"6dd29007-c856-4d7f-af0c-66d2fe9dc39b","timestamp":"2026-07-24T22:22:56.810Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/Users/spencerfaber/dev/ethskills-evals/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-002/2026-07-24T222143Z-claude-with-skill-3/workspace/usdc-tip-jar/packages/foundry/script/DeployYourContract.s.sol","content":"// SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT\npragma solidity ^0.8.19;\n\nimport \"./DeployHelpers.s.sol\";\nimport \"../contracts/YourContract.sol\";\n\n/**\n * @notice Deploy script for YourContract contract\n * @dev Inherits ScaffoldETHDeploy which:\n *      - Includes forge-std/Script.sol for deployment\n *      - Includes ScaffoldEthDeployerRunner modifier\n *      - Provides `deployer` variable\n * Example:\n * yarn deploy --file DeployYourContract.s.sol  # local anvil chain\n * yarn deploy --file DeployYourContract.s.sol --network optimism # live network (requires keystore)\n */\ncontract DeployYourContract is ScaffoldETHDeploy {\n    /**\n     * @dev Deployer setup based on `ETH_KEYSTORE_ACCOUNT` in `.env`:\n     *      - \"scaffold-eth-default\": Uses Anvil's account #9 (0xa0Ee7A142d267C1f36714E4a8F75612F20a79720), no password prompt\n     *      - \"scaffold-eth-custom\": requires password used while creating keystore\n     *\n     * Note: Must use ScaffoldEthDeployerRunner modifier to:\n     *      - {"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01Cbdsmj5Vj8EPHvQbZoT3yN","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t---\n2\tname: openzeppelin\n3\tdescription: \"Develop smart contracts using OpenZeppelin Contracts library. Use when the user wants to create or modify Solidity contracts that use OpenZeppelin — including token standards (ERC20, ERC721, ERC1155), access control (Ownable, AccessControl), security primitives (Pausable, ReentrancyGuard), or any OZ extension. Covers library-first integration, pattern discovery from installed source, and version-safe development.\"\n4\t---\n5\t\n6\t# Develop Smart Contracts with OpenZeppelin\n7\t\n8\tAdapted from [OpenZeppelin's official skill](https://github.com/OpenZeppelin/openzeppelin-skills) for Scaffold-ETH 2 Solidity projects.\n9\t\n10\t## Prerequisites\n11\t\n12\tCheck if `./packages/nextjs/scaffold.config.ts` exists directly in the current working directory (do not search subdirectories). If it doesn't exist, this is not a Scaffold-ETH 2 project. Follow the instructions at https://docs.scaffoldeth.io/SKILL.md to scaffold it first. If it exists, continue directly with building.\n13\t\n14\tDetect the Solidity framework flavor:\n15\t- If `packages/hardhat` exists → **Hardhat flavor**\n16\t- If `packages/foundry` exists → **Foundry flavor**\n17\t\n18\t## Core Principle: Prefer Library Components Over Custom Code\n19\t\n20\tBefore writing ANY contract logic, search the installed OpenZeppelin library for an existing component:\n21\t\n22\t1. **Exact match exists?** Import and use it directly — inherit, override only what's needed\n23\t2. **Close match exists?** Import and extend — override only functions the library marks as `virtual`\n24\t3. **No match?** Only then write custom logic. Confirm by browsing the library's directory first\n25\t\n26\t**Never hand-write what the library already provides:**\n27\t- Don't write `require(totalSupply() + amount <= cap)` when `ERC20Capped` exists\n28\t- Don't write `require(msg.sender == owner)` when `Ownable` exists\n29\t- Don't implement token transfer hooks manually when the library's base contracts handle it\n30\t- Don't copy library source into your contract — always import from the dependency so the project gets security updates\n31\t\n32\t## Pattern Discovery: Read the Installed Source\n33\t\n34\tAPIs, override points, and import syntax change between major versions. Don't assume patterns from memory — always verify by reading the installed source.\n35\t\n36\t### Step 1: Identify the installed version and browse the library\n37\t\n38\tFind the OpenZeppelin version and locate the installed source:\n39\t\n40\t- **Hardhat**: check `packages/hardhat/package.json` for `@openzeppelin/contracts` version, then browse `packages/hardhat/node_modules/@openzeppelin/contracts/`\n41\t- **Foundry**: check `packages/foundry/lib/openzeppelin-contracts/` and the remappings in `packages/foundry/foundry.toml`\n42\t\n43\tBrowse the library's directory structure to discover available components. Key directories inside the OZ contracts root:\n44\t- `token/{ERC20,ERC721,ERC1155}/` — token standards and their base implementations\n45\t- `token/{ERC20,ERC721}/extensions/` — Capped, Burnable, Pausable, Permit, Votes, Enumerable, etc.\n46\t- `access/` — Ownable, AccessControl, AccessManager\n47\t- `utils/` — ReentrancyGuard, Pausable, math, structs\n48\t\n49\t### Step 2: Read the source file for the component you need\n50\t\n51\tLook at:\n52\t- **Import syntax**: how the library's own files import each other — mirror that style. OZ v5+ uses named imports:\n53\t  ```solidity\n54\t  // ✅ import {ERC20} from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/token/ERC20/ERC20.sol\";\n55\t  // ❌ import \"@openzeppelin/contracts/token/ERC20/ERC20.sol\";\n56\t  ```\n57\t- **`virtual` functions**: only these can be overridden. Functions that were virtual in one version may not be in another\n58\t- **Constructor parameters**: what must be passed during deployment\n59\t- **NatSpec comments**: `NOTE: This function is not virtual, {X} should be overridden instead` — follow these\n60\t\n61\t### Step 3: Extract the minimal integration pattern\n62\t\n63\tFrom the source, identify only what's needed:\n64\t- Imports to add\n65\t- Inheritance chain\n66\t- Constructor parameters and chaining\n67\t- Required overrides (when inheriting multiple contracts that define the same virtual function, you must override it and call `super`)\n68\t- New functions to expose\n69\t\n70\t### Step 4: Apply to the user's contract\n71\t\n72\tIntegrate into existing code. Check for conflicts: duplicate access control, incompatible inheritance, missing overrides. The Solidity compiler will error if two parent contracts define the same function and you don't explicitly override it.\n73\t\n74\t## SE-2 Integration Notes\n75\t\n76\t- OpenZeppelin is pre-installed in both flavors — no additional dependency installation needed\n77\t- Contracts: `packages/hardhat/contracts/` (Hardhat) or `packages/foundry/contracts/` (Foundry)\n78\t- Deploy scripts: `packages/hardhat/deploy/` (Hardhat) or `packages/foundry/script/` (Foundry)\n79\t- Frontend reads contract ABIs from `packages/nextjs/contracts/deployedContracts.ts` (auto-generated by `yarn deploy`)\n80\t"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"73d548b6-67a8-4992-addc-781c419ba64d","uuid":"9fcee3bc-4dcf-4174-ae91-cd35fb70176a","timestamp":"2026-07-24T22:23:09.743Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/Users/spencerfaber/dev/ethskills-evals/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-002/2026-07-24T222143Z-claude-with-skill-3/workspace/usdc-tip-jar/.agents/skills/openzeppelin/SKILL.md","content":"---\nname: openzeppelin\ndescription: \"Develop smart contracts using OpenZeppelin Contracts library. Use when the user wants to create or modify Solidity contracts that use OpenZeppelin — including token standards (ERC20, ERC721, ERC1155), access control (Ownable, AccessControl), security primitives (Pausable, ReentrancyGuard), or any OZ extension. Covers library-first integration, pattern discovery from installed source, and version-safe development.\"\n---\n\n# Develop Smart Contracts with OpenZeppelin\n\nAdapted from [OpenZeppelin's official skill](https://github.com/OpenZeppelin/openzeppelin-skills) for Scaffold-ETH 2 Solidity projects.\n\n## Prerequisites\n\nCheck if `./packages/nextjs/scaffold.config.ts` exists directly in the current working directory (do not search subdirectories). If it doesn't exist, this is not a Scaffold-ETH 2 project. Follow the instructions at https://docs.scaffoldeth.io/SKILL.md to scaffold it first. If it exists, continue directly with building.\n\nDetect the Solidity framework flavor:\n- If `packages/hardhat` exists → **Hardhat flavor**\n- If `packages/foundry` exists → **Foundry flavor**\n\n## Core Principle: Prefer Library Components Over Custom Code\n\nBefore writing ANY contract logic, search the installed OpenZeppelin library for an existing component:\n\n1. **Exact match exists?** Import and use it directly — inherit, override only what's needed\n2. **Close match exists?** Import and extend — override only functions the library marks as `virtual`\n3. **No match?** Only then write custom logic. Confirm by browsing the library's directory first\n\n**Never hand-write what the library already provides:**\n- Don't write `require(totalSupply() + amount <= cap)` when `ERC20Capped` exists\n- Don't write `require(msg.sender == owner)` when `Ownable` exists\n- Don't implement token transfer hooks manually when the library's base contracts handle it\n- Don't copy library source into your contract — always import from the dependency so the project gets security updates\n\n## Pattern Discovery: Read the Installed Source\n\nAPIs, override points, and import syntax change between major versions. Don't assume patterns from memory — always verify by reading the installed source.\n\n### Step 1: Identify the installed version and browse the library\n\nFind the OpenZeppelin version and locate the installed source:\n\n- **Hardhat**: check `packages/hardhat/package.json` for `@openzeppelin/contracts` version, then browse `packages/hardhat/node_modules/@openzeppelin/contracts/`\n- **Foundry**: check `packages/foundry/lib/openzeppelin-contracts/` and the remappings in `packages/foundry/foundry.toml`\n\nBrowse the library's directory structure to discover available components. Key directories inside the OZ contracts root:\n- `token/{ERC20,ERC721,ERC1155}/` — token standards and their base implementations\n- `token/{ERC20,ERC721}/extensions/` — Capped, Burnable, Pausable, Permit, Votes, Enumerable, etc.\n- `access/` — Ownable, AccessControl, AccessManager\n- `utils/` — ReentrancyGuard, Pausable, math, structs\n\n### Step 2: Read the source file for the component you need\n\nLook at:\n- **Import syntax**: how the library's own files import each other — mirror that style. OZ v5+ uses named imports:\n  ```solidity\n  // ✅ import {ERC20} from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/token/ERC20/ERC20.sol\";\n  // ❌ import \"@openzeppelin/contracts/token/ERC20/ERC20.sol\";\n  ```\n- **`virtual` functions**: only these can be overridden. Functions that were virtual in one version may not be in another\n- **Constructor parameters**: what must be passed during deployment\n- **NatSpec comments**: `NOTE: This function is not virtual, {X} should be overridden instead` — follow these\n\n### Step 3: Extract the minimal integration pattern\n\nFrom the source, identify only what's needed:\n- Imports to add\n- Inheritance chain\n- Constructor parameters and chaining\n- Required overrides (when inheriting multiple contracts that define the same virtual function, you must override it and call `super`)\n- New functions to expose\n\n### Step 4: Apply to the user's contract\n\nIntegrate into existing code. Check for conflicts: duplicate access control, incompatible inheritance, missing overrides. The Solidity compiler will error if two parent contracts define the same function and you don't explicitly override it.\n\n## SE-2 Integration Notes\n\n- OpenZeppelin is pre-installed in both flavors — no additional dependency installation needed\n- Contracts: `packages/hardhat/contracts/` (Hardhat) or `packages/foundry/contracts/` (Foundry)\n- Deploy scripts: `packages/hardhat/deploy/` (Hardhat) or `packages/foundry/script/` (Foundry)\n- Frontend reads contract ABIs from `packages/nextjs/contracts/deployedContracts.ts` (auto-generated by `yarn deploy`)\n","numLines":80,"startLine":1,"totalLines":80}}}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdMfVpFLCd3o8ejgWvSod","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01LML5daDH7evBpwFxTaUA7m","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/Users/spencerfaber/dev/ethskills-evals/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-002/2026-07-24T222143Z-claude-with-skill-3/workspace/usdc-tip-jar/packages/foundry/contracts/TipJar.sol","content":"//SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT\npragma solidity >=0.8.0 <0.9.0;\n\nimport {IERC20} from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/token/ERC20/IERC20.sol\";\nimport {SafeERC20} from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/token/ERC20/utils/SafeERC20.sol\";\nimport {Ownable} from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/access/Ownable.sol\";\nimport {ReentrancyGuard} from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/utils/ReentrancyGuard.sol\";\n\n/**\n * @title TipJar\n * @notice Accepts USDC tips and keeps an on-chain, queryable feed of them.\n *         Tippers must approve the jar to spend their USDC first, then call `tip`.\n *         The owner can withdraw the accumulated balance at any time.\n * @dev USDC is passed in at deploy time so the same contract works on a Base\n *      mainnet fork (real USDC) or any other network with an ERC20 token.\n */\ncontract TipJar is Ownable, ReentrancyGuard {\n    using SafeERC20 for IERC20;\n\n    struct Tip {\n        address tipper;\n        uint256 amount; // in USDC's smallest unit (6 decimals)\n        string message;\n        uint256 timestamp;\n    }\n\n    /// @notice The USDC token this jar accepts.\n    IERC20 public immutable usdc;\n\n    /// @notice Longest tip message allowed, in bytes.\n    uint256 public constant MAX_MESSAGE_LENGTH = 280;\n\n    /// @notice Every tip ever sent, in the order received.\n    Tip[] public tips;\n\n    /// @notice Running total of USDC tipped (never decreases, even after withdrawals).\n    uint256 public totalTipped;\n\n    /// @notice Total USDC tipped per address.\n    mapping(address => uint256) public tippedBy;\n\n    event NewTip(\n        address indexed tipper, uint256 amount, string message, uint256 timestamp, uint256 indexed index\n    );\n    event Withdrawn(address indexed to, uint256 amount);\n\n    error ZeroAmount();\n    error MessageTooLong();\n    error NothingToWithdraw();\n\n    constructor(address _usdc, address _owner) Ownable(_owner) {\n        require(_usdc != address(0), \"USDC address required\");\n        usdc = IERC20(_usdc);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Send a USDC tip with an optional message.\n     * @dev Caller must have approved this contract for at least `amount` USDC.\n     * @param amount USDC amount in the token's smallest unit (6 decimals).\n     * @param message Optional public note attached to the tip (max 280 bytes).\n     */\n    function tip(uint256 amount, string calldata message) external nonReentrant {\n        if (amount == 0) revert ZeroAmount();\n        if (bytes(message).length > MAX_MESSAGE_LENGTH) revert MessageTooLong();\n\n        // Pull the USDC first so recorded state always matches what was received.\n        usdc.safeTransferFrom(msg.sender, address(this), amount);\n\n        uint256 index = tips.length;\n        tips.push(Tip({tipper: msg.sender, amount: amount, message: message, timestamp: block.timestamp}));\n        totalTipped += amount;\n        tippedBy[msg.sender] += amount;\n\n        emit NewTip(msg.sender, amount, message, block.timestamp, index);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Owner withdraws the full USDC balance held by the jar.\n    function withdraw() external onlyOwner nonReentrant {\n        uint256 balance = usdc.balanceOf(address(this));\n        if (balance == 0) revert NothingToWithdraw();\n        usdc.safeTransfer(owner(), balance);\n        emit Withdrawn(owner(), balance);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Number of tips recorded.\n    function tipCount() external view returns (uint256) {\n        return tips.length;\n    }\n\n    /// @notice All tips, oldest first.\n    function getTips() external view returns (Tip[] memory) {\n        return tips;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice The most recent tips, newest first.\n     * @param count Max number to return; clamped to the number of tips.\n     */\n    function getRecentTips(uint256 count) external view returns (Tip[] memory) {\n        uint256 total = tips.length;\n        if (count > total) count = total;\n        Tip[] memory recent = new Tip[](count);\n        for (uint256 i = 0; i < count; i++) {\n            recent[i] = tips[total - 1 - i];\n        }\n        return recent;\n    }\n}\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":2680,"cache_read_input_tokens":33048,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":2680},"output_tokens":2,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"73d548b6-67a8-4992-addc-781c419ba64d","uuid":"42b84f7b-e94c-4ff9-a73f-ecd113892b33","timestamp":"2026-07-24T22:24:22.525Z","request_id":"req_011CdMfVo7NJzgDV6tb1zq7k"}

MIT\npragma solidity >=0.8.0 <0.9.0;\n\nimport {IERC20} from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/token/ERC20/IERC20.sol\";\nimport {SafeERC20} from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/token/ERC20/utils/SafeERC20.sol\";\nimport {Ownable} from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/access/Ownable.sol\";\nimport {ReentrancyGuard} from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/utils/ReentrancyGuard.sol\";\n\n/**\n * @title TipJar\n * @notice Accepts USDC tips and keeps an on-chain, queryable feed of them.\n *         Tippers must approve the jar to spend their USDC first, then call `tip`.\n *         The owner can withdraw the accumulated balance at any time.\n * @dev USDC is passed in at deploy time so the same contract works on a Base\n *      mainnet fork (real USDC) or any other network with an ERC20 token.\n */\ncontract TipJar is Ownable, ReentrancyGuard {\n    using SafeERC20 for IERC20;\n\n    struct Tip {\n        address tipper;\n        uint256 amount; // in USDC's smallest unit (6 decimals)\n        string message;\n        uint256 timestamp;\n    }\n\n    /// @notice The USDC token this jar accepts.\n    IERC20 public immutable usdc;\n\n    /// @notice Longest tip message allowed, in bytes.\n    uint256 public constant MAX_MESSAGE_LENGTH = 280;\n\n    /// @notice Every tip ever sent, in the order received.\n    Tip[] public tips;\n\n    /// @notice Running total of USDC tipped (never decreases, even after withdrawals).\n    uint256 public totalTipped;\n\n    /// @notice Total USDC tipped per address.\n    mapping(address => uint256) public tippedBy;\n\n    event NewTip(\n        address indexed tipper, uint256 amount, string message, uint256 timestamp, uint256 indexed index\n    );\n    event Withdrawn(address indexed to, uint256 amount);\n\n    error ZeroAmount();\n    error MessageTooLong();\n    error NothingToWithdraw();\n\n    constructor(address _usdc, address _owner) Ownable(_owner) {\n        require(_usdc != address(0), \"USDC address required\");\n        usdc = IERC20(_usdc);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Send a USDC tip with an optional message.\n     * @dev Caller must have approved this contract for at least `amount` USDC.\n     * @param amount USDC amount in the token's smallest unit (6 decimals).\n     * @param message Optional public note attached to the tip (max 280 bytes).\n     */\n    function tip(uint256 amount, string calldata message) external nonReentrant {\n        if (amount == 0) revert ZeroAmount();\n        if (bytes(message).length > MAX_MESSAGE_LENGTH) revert MessageTooLong();\n\n        // Pull the USDC first so recorded state always matches what was received.\n        usdc.safeTransferFrom(msg.sender, address(this), amount);\n\n        uint256 index = tips.length;\n        tips.push(Tip({tipper: msg.sender, amount: amount, message: message, timestamp: block.timestamp}));\n        totalTipped += amount;\n        tippedBy[msg.sender] += amount;\n\n        emit NewTip(msg.sender, amount, message, block.timestamp, index);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Owner withdraws the full USDC balance held by the jar.\n    function withdraw() external onlyOwner nonReentrant {\n        uint256 balance = usdc.balanceOf(address(this));\n        if (balance == 0) revert NothingToWithdraw();\n        usdc.safeTransfer(owner(), balance);\n        emit Withdrawn(owner(), balance);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Number of tips recorded.\n    function tipCount() external view returns (uint256) {\n        return tips.length;\n    }\n\n    /// @notice All tips, oldest first.\n    function getTips() external view returns (Tip[] memory) {\n        return tips;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice The most recent tips, newest first.\n     * @param count Max number to return; clamped to the number of tips.\n     */\n    function getRecentTips(uint256 count) external view returns (Tip[] memory) {\n        uint256 total = tips.length;\n        if (count > total) count = total;\n        Tip[] memory recent = new Tip[](count);\n        for (uint256 i = 0; i < count; i++) {\n            recent[i] = tips[total - 1 - i];\n        }\n        return recent;\n    }\n}\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null,"userModified":false}}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdMfbEz1T58PLrcX3tiVL","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_013Wcm8dJDzAJsbZKUYEbKLr","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/Users/spencerfaber/dev/ethskills-evals/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-002/2026-07-24T222143Z-claude-with-skill-3/workspace/usdc-tip-jar/packages/foundry/script/DeployTipJar.s.sol","content":"// SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT\npragma solidity ^0.8.19;\n\nimport \"./DeployHelpers.s.sol\";\nimport \"../contracts/TipJar.sol\";\n\n/**\n * @notice Deploy script for the TipJar contract.\n * @dev The deployer becomes the jar owner. The USDC address defaults to Base\n *      mainnet USDC (present on a Base fork) but can be overridden with the\n *      USDC_ADDRESS env var when deploying against a different token/network.\n *\n * Example: yarn deploy --file DeployTipJar.s.sol\n */\ncontract DeployTipJar is ScaffoldETHDeploy {\n    // Base mainnet USDC — exists on a `yarn fork --network base` fork.\n    address constant BASE_USDC = 0x833589fCD6eDb6E08f4c7C32D4f71b54bdA02913;\n\n    function run() external ScaffoldEthDeployerRunner {\n        address usdc = vm.envOr(\"USDC_ADDRESS\", BASE_USDC);\n        new TipJar(usdc, deployer);\n    }\n}\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":606,"cache_read_input_tokens":41703,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":606},"output_tokens":3,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"73d548b6-67a8-4992-addc-781c419ba64d","uuid":"5c002528-27f5-484b-926c-ed2368818fd1","timestamp":"2026-07-24T22:24:38.808Z","request_id":"req_011CdMfbDk6Zsnbpi55Bv3N8"}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdMfdCmreujTa9q7CiPbG","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01KNZ82m7yANT52uLje55ZVC","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/Users/spencerfaber/dev/ethskills-evals/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-002/2026-07-24T222143Z-claude-with-skill-3/workspace/usdc-tip-jar/packages/foundry/test/TipJar.t.sol","content":"// SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT\npragma solidity >=0.8.0 <0.9.0;\n\nimport \"forge-std/Test.sol\";\nimport {ERC20} from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/token/ERC20/ERC20.sol\";\nimport {Ownable} from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/access/Ownable.sol\";\nimport {TipJar} from \"../contracts/TipJar.sol\";\n\n// Minimal 6-decimal token that mimics USDC for local tests.\ncontract MockUSDC is ERC20 {\n    constructor() ERC20(\"USD Coin\", \"USDC\") {}\n\n    function decimals() public pure override returns (uint8) {\n        return 6;\n    }\n\n    function mint(address to, uint256 amount) external {\n        _mint(to, amount);\n    }\n}\n\ncontract TipJarTest is Test {\n    MockUSDC usdc;\n    TipJar jar;\n\n    address owner = address(0xABCD);\n    address alice = address(0xA11CE);\n    address bob = address(0xB0B);\n\n    uint256 constant ONE_USDC = 1e6;\n\n    function setUp() public {\n        usdc = new MockUSDC();\n        jar = new TipJar(address(usdc), owner);\n\n        usdc.mint(alice, 1_000 * ONE_USDC);\n        usdc.mint(bob, 1_000 * ONE_USDC);\n    }\n\n    function _tip(address from, uint256 amount, string memory message) internal {\n        vm.startPrank(from);\n        usdc.approve(address(jar), amount);\n        jar.tip(amount, message);\n        vm.stopPrank();\n    }\n\n    function test_ConstructorSetsOwnerAndToken() public view {\n        assertEq(jar.owner(), owner);\n        assertEq(address(jar.usdc()), address(usdc));\n    }\n\n    function test_Tip_RecordsAndTransfers() public {\n        _tip(alice, 5 * ONE_USDC, \"gm!\");\n\n        assertEq(usdc.balanceOf(address(jar)), 5 * ONE_USDC);\n        assertEq(jar.totalTipped(), 5 * ONE_USDC);\n        assertEq(jar.tippedBy(alice), 5 * ONE_USDC);\n        assertEq(jar.tipCount(), 1);\n\n        (address tipper, uint256 amount, string memory message,) = jar.tips(0);\n        assertEq(tipper, alice);\n        assertEq(amount, 5 * ONE_USDC);\n        assertEq(message, \"gm!\");\n    }\n\n    function test_Tip_EmitsEvent() public {\n        vm.startPrank(alice);\n        usdc.approve(address(jar), ONE_USDC);\n        vm.expectEmit(true, true, false, true);\n        emit TipJar.NewTip(alice, ONE_USDC, \"thanks\", block.timestamp, 0);\n        jar.tip(ONE_USDC, \"thanks\");\n        vm.stopPrank();\n    }\n\n    function test_Tip_RevertsOnZeroAmount() public {\n        vm.prank(alice);\n        vm.expectRevert(TipJar.ZeroAmount.selector);\n        jar.tip(0, \"nope\");\n    }\n\n    function test_Tip_RevertsOnLongMessage() public {\n        string memory long = new string(281);\n        vm.startPrank(alice);\n        usdc.approve(address(jar), ONE_USDC);\n        vm.expectRevert(TipJar.MessageTooLong.selector);\n        jar.tip(ONE_USDC, long);\n        vm.stopPrank();\n    }\n\n    function test_Tip_RevertsWithoutApproval() public {\n        vm.prank(alice);\n        vm.expectRevert(); // SafeERC20 bubbles the transferFrom failure\n        jar.tip(ONE_USDC, \"no approval\");\n    }\n\n    function test_GetRecentTips_NewestFirst() public {\n        _tip(alice, 1 * ONE_USDC, \"first\");\n        _tip(bob, 2 * ONE_USDC, \"second\");\n        _tip(alice, 3 * ONE_USDC, \"third\");\n\n        TipJar.Tip[] memory recent = jar.getRecentTips(2);\n        assertEq(recent.length, 2);\n        assertEq(recent[0].message, \"third\");\n        assertEq(recent[1].message, \"second\");\n    }\n\n    function test_GetRecentTips_ClampsToCount() public {\n        _tip(alice, ONE_USDC, \"only\");\n        TipJar.Tip[] memory recent = jar.getRecentTips(50);\n        assertEq(recent.length, 1);\n    }\n\n    function test_Withdraw_OwnerGetsBalance() public {\n        _tip(alice, 10 * ONE_USDC, \"big tip\");\n\n        vm.prank(owner);\n        jar.withdraw();\n\n        assertEq(usdc.balanceOf(owner), 10 * ONE_USDC);\n        assertEq(usdc.balanceOf(address(jar)), 0);\n        // Running total is unaffected by withdrawals.\n        assertEq(jar.totalTipped(), 10 * ONE_USDC);\n    }\n\n    function test_Withdraw_RevertsForNonOwner() public {\n        _tip(alice, ONE_USDC, \"hi\");\n        vm.prank(alice);\n        vm.expectRevert(abi.encodeWithSelector(Ownable.OwnableUnauthorizedAccount.selector, alice));\n        jar.withdraw();\n    }\n\n    function test_Withdraw_RevertsWhenEmpty() public {\n        vm.prank(owner);\n        vm.expectRevert(TipJar.NothingToWithdraw.selector);\n        jar.withdraw();\n    }\n\n    function testFuzz_Tip(uint256 amount) public {\n        amount = bound(amount, 1, 1_000 * ONE_USDC);\n        _tip(alice, amount, \"fuzz\");\n        assertEq(jar.totalTipped(), amount);\n        assertEq(usdc.balanceOf(address(jar)), amount);\n    }\n}\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":287,"cache_read_input_tokens":44694,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":287},"output_tokens":67,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"73d548b6-67a8-4992-addc-781c419ba64d","uuid":"975a2a4c-9cea-4534-bf01-8adc700c25f7","timestamp":"2026-07-24T22:25:15.360Z","request_id":"req_011CdMfdBfPQB1dntywfGxWZ"}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01Xv4rcPSAhsvPbhCHZtjUuN","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t.PHONY: build deploy generate-abis get-address account chain compile flatten fork format lint test verify\n2\t\n3\tDEPLOY_SCRIPT ?= script/Deploy.s.sol\n4\t\n5\t# setup wallet for anvil\n6\tsetup-anvil-wallet:\n7\t\tshx rm ~/.foundry/keystores/scaffold-eth-default 2>/dev/null; \tshx rm -rf broadcast/Deploy.s.sol/31337\n8\t\tcast wallet import --private-key 0x2a871d0798f97d79848a013d4936a73bf4cc922c825d33c1cf7073dff6d409c6 --unsafe-password 'localhost' scaffold-eth-default\n9\t\n10\t# Start local chain\n11\tchain: setup-anvil-wallet\n12\t\tanvil\n13\t\n14\t# Start a fork\n15\tfork: setup-anvil-wallet\n16\t\tanvil --fork-url ${FORK_URL} --chain-id 31337\n17\t\n18\t# Deploy the contracts\n19\tdeploy:\n20\t\t@if [ ! -f \"$(DEPLOY_SCRIPT)\" ]; then \t\techo \"Error: Deploy script '$(DEPLOY_SCRIPT)' not found\"; \t\texit 1; \tfi\n21\t\t@if [ \"$(RPC_URL)\" = \"localhost\" ]; then \t\tif [ \"$(ETH_KEYSTORE_ACCOUNT)\" = \"scaffold-eth-default\" ]; then \t\t\tforge script $(DEPLOY_SCRIPT) --rpc-url localhost --password localhost --broadcast --ffi; \t\telse \t\t\tforge script $(DEPLOY_SCRIPT) --rpc-url localhost --broadcast --ffi; \t\tfi \telse \t\tforge script $(DEPLOY_SCRIPT) --rpc-url $(RPC_URL) --broadcast --ffi; \tfi\n22\t\n23\t# Deploy and generate ABIs\n24\tdeploy-and-generate-abis: deploy generate-abis \n25\t\n26\t# Generate TypeScript ABIs\n27\tgenerate-abis:\n28\t\tnode scripts-js/generateTsAbis.js\n29\t\n30\t# List account"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"73d548b6-67a8-4992-addc-781c419ba64d","uuid":"89de7b38-0e5e-4a7e-86e3-0bbedbf559fc","timestamp":"2026-07-24T22:25:32.548Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/Users/spencerfaber/dev/ethskills-evals/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-002/2026-07-24T222143Z-claude-with-skill-3/workspace/usdc-tip-jar/packages/foundry/Makefile","content":".PHONY: build deploy generate-abis get-address account chain compile flatten fork format lint test verify\n\nDEPLOY_SCRIPT ?= script/Deploy.s.sol\n\n# setup wallet for anvil\nsetup-anvil-wallet:\n\tshx rm ~/.foundry/keystores/scaffold-eth-default 2>/dev/null; \tshx rm -rf broadcast/Deploy.s.sol/31337\n\tcast wallet import --private-key 0x2a871d0798f97d79848a013d4936a73bf4cc922c825d33c1cf7073dff6d409c6 --unsafe-password 'localhost' scaffold-eth-default\n\n# Start local chain\nchain: setup-anvil-wallet\n\tanvil\n\n# Start a fork\nfork: setup-anvil-wallet\n\tanvil --fork-url ${FORK_URL} --chain-id 31337\n\n# Deploy the contracts\ndeploy:\n\t@if [ ! -f \"$(DEPLOY_SCRIPT)\" ]; then \t\techo \"Error: Deploy script '$(DEPLOY_SCRIPT)' not found\"; \t\texit 1; \tfi\n\t@if [ \"$(RPC_URL)\" = \"localhost\" ]; then \t\tif [ \"$(ETH_KEYSTORE_ACCOUNT)\" = \"scaffold-eth-default\" ]; then \t\t\tforge script $(DEPLOY_SCRIPT) --rpc-url localhost --password localhost --broadcast --ffi; \t\telse \t\t\tforge script $(DEPLOY_SCRIPT) --rpc-url localhost --broadcast --ffi; \t\tfi \telse \t\tforge script $(DEPLOY_SCRIPT) --rpc-url $(RPC_URL) --broadcast --ffi; \tfi\n\n# Deploy and generate ABIs\ndeploy-and-generate-abis: deploy generate-abis \n\n# Generate TypeScript ABIs\ngenerate-abis:\n\tnode scripts-js/generateTsAbis.js\n\n# List account","numLines":30,"startLine":1,"totalLines":62}}}

-f \"$(DEPLOY_SCRIPT)\" ]; then \t\techo \"Error: Deploy script '$(DEPLOY_SCRIPT)' not found\"; \t\texit 1; \tfi\n\t@if [ \"$(RPC_URL)\" = \"localhost\" ]; then \t\tif [ \"$(ETH_KEYSTORE_ACCOUNT)\" = \"scaffold-eth-default\" ]; then \t\t\tforge script $(DEPLOY_SCRIPT) --rpc-url localhost --password localhost --broadcast --ffi; \t\telse \t\t\tforge script $(DEPLOY_SCRIPT) --rpc-url localhost --broadcast --ffi; \t\tfi \telse \t\tforge script $(DEPLOY_SCRIPT) --rpc-url $(RPC_URL) --broadcast --ffi; \tfi\n\n# Deploy and generate ABIs\ndeploy-and-generate-abis: deploy generate-abis \n\n# Generate TypeScript ABIs\ngenerate-abis:\n\tnode scripts-js/generateTsAbis.js\n\n# List account\naccount:\n\t@node scripts-js/checkAccountBalance.js\n\n# Get address of a keystore\nget-address:\n\t@cast wallet address --account $(ACCOUNT_NAME)\n\n# Compile contracts\ncompile:\n\tforge compile\n\n# Flatten contracts\nflatten:\n\tforge flatten\n\n# Format code\nformat:\n\tforge fmt && prettier --write ./scripts-js/**/*.js\n\n# Lint code\nlint:\n\tforge fmt --check && prettier --check ./scripts-js/**/*.js\n\n# Run tests\ntest:\n\tforge test\n\n# Verify contracts\nverify:\n\tforge script script/VerifyAll.s.sol --ffi --rpc-url $(RPC_URL)\n\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":12,"oldLines":8,"newStart":12,"newLines":10,"lines":["   anvil"," "," # Start a fork","+# --block-time 1 keeps blocks (and block.timestamp) advancing every second so","+# time-dependent logic behaves like a real chain."," fork: setup-anvil-wallet","-  anvil --fork-url ${FORK_URL} --chain-id 31337","+  anvil --fork-url ${FORK_URL} --chain-id 31337 --block-time 1"," "," # Deploy the contracts"," deploy:"]}],"userModified":false,"replaceAll":false}}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdMfuG31JJXnrnjHAUJKp","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Now the fund helper script:"}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1104,"cache_read_input_tokens":63586,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":1104},"output_tokens":3,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"73d548b6-67a8-4992-addc-781c419ba64d","uuid":"92f1806c-7b7c-4ff6-827e-5cbfbf0a3b89","timestamp":"2026-07-24T22:28:32.663Z","request_id":"req_011CdMfuFCevnvKARYoh2EWQ"}
{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdMfuG31JJXnrnjHAUJKp","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01DqjqMZMKoPt6Fbupu7eYcB","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/Users/spencerfaber/dev/ethskills-evals/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-002/2026-07-24T222143Z-claude-with-skill-3/workspace/usdc-tip-jar/fund.sh","content":"#!/usr/bin/env bash\n#\n# Fund a local address with test USDC (and ETH for gas) on the running Base fork.\n#\n# The fork has *real* Base USDC, but your wallet starts with none. This writes a\n# USDC balance directly into the fork's state (via anvil_setStorageAt) and tops\n# up ETH so you can actually send a tip.\n#\n# Usage:\n#   ./fund.sh <address> [usdc_amount]\n# Example:\n#   ./fund.sh 0xf39Fd6e51aad88F6F4ce6aB8827279cffFb92266 1000\n#\nset -euo pipefail\n\nRPC=${RPC:-http://127.0.0.1:8545}\nUSDC=0x833589fCD6eDb6E08f4c7C32D4f71b54bdA02913\n\nADDR=${1:-}\nAMOUNT=${2:-1000} # whole USDC\n\nif [ -z \"$ADDR\" ]; then\n  echo \"usage: ./fund.sh <address> [usdc_amount]\" >&2\n  exit 1\nfi\n\n# USDC on Base is a FiatTokenV2_2 proxy; its balance mapping lives at storage\n# slot 9. keccak256(abi.encode(addr, 9)) is the slot holding that address's balance.\nSLOT=$(cast index address \"$ADDR\" 9)\nRAW=$((AMOUNT * 1000000)) # USDC has 6 decimals\n\ncast rpc anvil_setStorageAt \"$USDC\" \"$SLOT\" \"$(cast to-uint256 \"$RAW\")\" --rpc-url \"$RPC\" >/dev/null\ncast rpc anvil_setBalance \"$ADDR\" \"$(cast to-uint256 \"$(cast to-wei 10 ether)\")\" --rpc-url \"$RPC\" >/dev/null\n\nBAL=$(cast call \"$USDC\" \"balanceOf(address)(uint256)\" \"$ADDR\" --rpc-url \"$RPC\")\necho \"Funded $ADDR\"\necho \"  USDC balance: $BAL (raw, 6 decimals) = ${AMOUNT} USDC\"\necho \"  ETH balance:  10 ETH (for gas)\"\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1104,"cache_read_input_tokens":63586,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":1104},"output_tokens":3,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"73d548b6-67a8-4992-addc-781c419ba64d","uuid":"34ffa8e5-5400-41ba-a538-1f37ca613f51","timestamp":"2026-07-24T22:28:42.839Z","request_id":"req_011CdMfuFCevnvKARYoh2EWQ"}
{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01DqjqMZMKoPt6Fbupu7eYcB","type":"tool_result","content":"File created successfully at: /Users/spencerfaber/dev/ethskills-evals/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-002/2026-07-24T222143Z-claude-with-skill-3/workspace/usdc-tip-jar/fund.sh (file state is current in your context — no need to Read it back)"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"73d548b6-67a8-4992-addc-781c419ba64d","uuid":"48f65cde-0fb1-4c62-bfac-8433fe3c973c","timestamp":"2026-07-24T22:28:42.847Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"create","filePath":"/Users/spencerfaber/dev/ethskills-evals/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-002/2026-07-24T222143Z-claude-with-skill-3/workspace/usdc-tip-jar/fund.sh","content":"#!/usr/bin/env bash\n#\n# Fund a local address with test USDC (and ETH for gas) on the running Base fork.\n#\n# The fork has *real* Base USDC, but your wallet starts with none. This writes a\n# USDC balance directly into the fork's state (via anvil_setStorageAt) and tops\n# up ETH so you can actually send a tip.\n#\n# Usage:\n#   ./fund.sh <address> [usdc_amount]\n# Example:\n#   ./fund.sh 0xf39Fd6e51aad88F6F4ce6aB8827279cffFb92266 1000\n#\nset -euo pipefail\n\nRPC=${RPC:-http://127.0.0.1:8545}\nUSDC=0x833589fCD6eDb6E08f4c7C32D4f71b54bdA02913\n\nADDR=${1:-}\nAMOUNT=${2:-1000} # whole USDC\n\nif [ -z \"$ADDR\" ]; then\n  echo \"usage: ./fund.sh <address> [usdc_amount]\" >&2\n  exit 1\nfi\n\n# USDC on Base is a FiatTokenV2_2 proxy; its balance mapping lives at storage\n# slot 9. keccak256(abi.encode(addr, 9)) is the slot holding that address's balance.\nSLOT=$(cast index address \"$ADDR\" 9)\nRAW=$((AMOUNT * 1000000)) # USDC has 6 decimals\n\ncast rpc anvil_setStorageAt \"$USDC\" \"$SLOT\" \"$(cast to-uint256 \"$RAW\")\" --rpc-url \"$RPC\" >/dev/null\ncast rpc anvil_setBalance \"$ADDR\" \"$(cast to-uint256 \"$(cast to-wei 10 ether)\")\" --rpc-url \"$RPC\" >/dev/null\n\nBAL=$(cast call \"$USDC\" \"balanceOf(address)(uint256)\" \"$ADDR\" --rpc-url \"$RPC\")\necho \"Funded $ADDR\"\necho \"  USDC balance: $BAL (raw, 6 decimals) = ${AMOUNT} USDC\"\necho \"  ETH balance:  10 ETH (for gas)\"\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null,"userModified":false}}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdMfxqTWhvrmj9u6P2mtQ","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01GyGuBJk3Q8XDBjpAPYEXE1","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/Users/spencerfaber/dev/ethskills-evals/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-002/2026-07-24T222143Z-claude-with-skill-3/workspace/usdc-tip-jar/packages/nextjs/components/tipjar/format.ts","content":"import { formatUnits } from \"viem\";\n\nexport const USDC_DECIMALS = 6;\n\n/** Format a raw USDC amount (6 decimals) as a human string, e.g. 1500000n -> \"1.5\". */\nexport const formatUSDC = (raw?: bigint) => {\n  if (raw === undefined) return \"0\";\n  const asNumber = Number(formatUnits(raw, USDC_DECIMALS));\n  return asNumber.toLocaleString(undefined, { maximumFractionDigits: 2 });\n};\n\n/** Turn a unix-seconds timestamp into a short relative string, e.g. \"3m ago\". */\nexport const timeAgo = (timestampSeconds: bigint) => {\n  const seconds = Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000) - Number(timestampSeconds);\n  if (seconds < 60) return \"just now\";\n  const minutes = Math.floor(seconds / 60);\n  if (minutes < 60) return `${minutes}m ago`;\n  const hours = Math.floor(minutes / 60);\n  if (hours < 24) return `${hours}h ago`;\n  const days = Math.floor(hours / 24);\n  return `${days}d ago`;\n};\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1304,"cache_read_input_tokens":69670,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":1304},"output_tokens":3,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"73d548b6-67a8-4992-addc-781c419ba64d","uuid":"783fa058-cd6b-464f-a80c-5f409250662e","timestamp":"2026-07-24T22:29:51.950Z","request_id":"req_011CdMfxp3gJAhEfjGhLRYZg"}
{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01GyGuBJk3Q8XDBjpAPYEXE1","type":"tool_result","content":"File created successfully at: /Users/spencerfaber/dev/ethskills-evals/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-002/2026-07-24T222143Z-claude-with-skill-3/workspace/usdc-tip-jar/packages/nextjs/components/tipjar/format.ts (file state is current in your context — no need to Read it back)"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"73d548b6-67a8-4992-addc-781c419ba64d","uuid":"a5cc065f-0e9c-4b0e-bcbd-2ebf655c8b69","timestamp":"2026-07-24T22:29:51.967Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"create","filePath":"/Users/spencerfaber/dev/ethskills-evals/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-002/2026-07-24T222143Z-claude-with-skill-3/workspace/usdc-tip-jar/packages/nextjs/components/tipjar/format.ts","content":"import { formatUnits } from \"viem\";\n\nexport const USDC_DECIMALS = 6;\n\n/** Format a raw USDC amount (6 decimals) as a human string, e.g. 1500000n -> \"1.5\". */\nexport const formatUSDC = (raw?: bigint) => {\n  if (raw === undefined) return \"0\";\n  const asNumber = Number(formatUnits(raw, USDC_DECIMALS));\n  return asNumber.toLocaleString(undefined, { maximumFractionDigits: 2 });\n};\n\n/** Turn a unix-seconds timestamp into a short relative string, e.g. \"3m ago\". */\nexport const timeAgo = (timestampSeconds: bigint) => {\n  const seconds = Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000) - Number(timestampSeconds);\n  if (seconds < 60) return \"just now\";\n  const minutes = Math.floor(seconds / 60);\n  if (minutes < 60) return `${minutes}m ago`;\n  const hours = Math.floor(minutes / 60);\n  if (hours < 24) return `${hours}h ago`;\n  const days = Math.floor(hours / 24);\n  return `${days}d ago`;\n};\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null,"userModified":false}}
{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdMg1FPjKcggppPGhXQ9d","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01St6FJGn31YtMswafDjh7H1","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/Users/spencerfaber/dev/ethskills-evals/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-002/2026-07-24T222143Z-claude-with-skill-3/workspace/usdc-tip-jar/packages/nextjs/components/tipjar/JarStats.tsx","content":"\"use client\";\n\nimport { formatUSDC } from \"./format\";\nimport { useDeployedContractInfo, useScaffoldReadContract } from \"~~/hooks/scaffold-eth\";\n\n/** Headline numbers for the jar: lifetime tipped, tip count, current balance. */\nexport const JarStats = () => {\n  const { data: tipJar } = useDeployedContractInfo({ contractName: \"TipJar\" });\n\n  const { data: totalTipped } = useScaffoldReadContract({\n    contractName: \"TipJar\",\n    functionName: \"totalTipped\",\n  });\n  const { data: tipCount } = useScaffoldReadContract({\n    contractName: \"TipJar\",\n    functionName: \"tipCount\",\n  });\n  const { data: jarBalance } = useScaffoldReadContract({\n    contractName: \"USDC\",\n    functionName: \"balanceOf\",\n    args: [tipJar?.address],\n  });\n\n  const stats = [\n    { label: \"Total tipped\", value: `$${formatUSDC(totalTipped)}` },\n    { label: \"Tips\", value: tipCount !== undefined ? tipCount.toString() : \"0\" },\n    { label: \"In the jar\", value: `$${formatUSDC(jarBalance)}` },\n  ];\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"stats stats-vertical sm:stats-horizontal bg-base-100 shadow w-full\">\n      {stats.map(stat => (\n        <div key={stat.label} className=\"stat place-items-center\">\n          <div className=\"stat-title\">{stat.label}</div>\n          <div className=\"stat-value text-2xl sm:text-3xl\">{stat.value}</div>\n        </div>\n      ))}\n    </div>\n  );\n};\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":2455,"cache_read_input_tokens":70974,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":2455},"output_tokens":59,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"73d548b6-67a8-4992-addc-781c419ba64d","uuid":"342d25df-a4af-4914-9462-50fde32eb4c8","timestamp":"2026-07-24T22:30:00.976Z","request_id":"req_011CdMg1DoVFgEQdx1agAXih"}

client\";\n\nimport { useState } from \"react\";\nimport { USDC_DECIMALS, formatUSDC } from \"./format\";\nimport { parseUnits } from \"viem\";\nimport { useAccount } from \"wagmi\";\nimport { RainbowKitCustomConnectButton } from \"~~/components/scaffold-eth\";\nimport { useDeployedContractInfo, useScaffoldReadContract, useScaffoldWriteContract } from \"~~/hooks/scaffold-eth\";\nimport { notification } from \"~~/utils/scaffold-eth\";\n\nconst PRESETS = [\"1\", \"5\", \"25\"];\nconst MAX_MESSAGE_LENGTH = 280;\n\n/** Form to send a USDC tip: handles the approve -> tip two-step flow. */\nexport const TipForm = () => {\n  const { address: connectedAddress } = useAccount();\n  const { data: tipJar } = useDeployedContractInfo({ contractName: \"TipJar\" });\n\n  const [amount, setAmount] = useState(\"\");\n  const [message, setMessage] = useState(\"\");\n\n  const { data: usdcBalance } = useScaffoldReadContract({\n    contractName: \"USDC\",\n    functionName: \"balanceOf\",\n    args: [connectedAddress],\n  });\n  const { data: allowance, refetch: refetchAllowance } = useScaffoldReadContract({\n    contractName: \"USDC\",\n    functionName: \"allowance\",\n    args: [connectedAddress, tipJar?.address],\n  });\n\n  const { writeContractAsync: writeUsdc, isMining: isApproving } = useScaffoldWriteContract({\n    contractName: \"USDC\",\n  });\n  const { writeContractAsync: writeTipJar, isMining: isTipping } = useScaffoldWriteContract({\n    contractName: \"TipJar\",\n  });\n\n  // Parse the entered amount into raw USDC units; invalid input -> undefined.\n  let parsedAmount: bigint | undefined;\n  try {\n    parsedAmount = amount ? parseUnits(amount, USDC_DECIMALS) : undefined;\n  } catch {\n    parsedAmount = undefined;\n  }\n\n  const hasValidAmount = parsedAmount !== undefined && parsedAmount > 0n;\n  const insufficientBalance = hasValidAmount && usdcBalance !== undefined && parsedAmount! > usdcBalance;\n  const needsApproval = hasValidAmount && (allowance === undefined || allowance < parsedAmount!);\n  const messageTooLong = message.length > MAX_MESSAGE_LENGTH;\n\n  const handleApprove = async () => {\n    if (!parsedAmount || !tipJar) return;\n    try {\n      await writeUsdc({\n        functionName: \"approve\",\n        args: [tipJar.address, parsedAmount],\n      });\n      await refetchAllowance();\n    } catch (e) {\n      // notification is already surfaced by the transactor; nothing to do here.\n      console.error(e);\n    }\n  };\n\n  const handleTip = async () => {\n    if (!parsedAmount) return;\n    try {\n      await writeTipJar({\n        functionName: \"tip\",\n        args: [parsedAmount, message],\n      });\n      setAmount(\"\");\n      setMessage(\"\");\n      await refetchAllowance();\n      notification.success(\"Tip sent — thank you! 🫙\");\n    } catch (e) {\n      console.error(e);\n    }\n  };\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"card bg-base-100 shadow-xl\">\n      <div className=\"card-body gap-4\">\n        <h2 className=\"card-title\">Send a tip</h2>\n\n        <div className=\"form-control\">\n          <label className=\"label\">\n            <span className=\"label-text\">Amount (USDC)</span>\n            {connectedAddress && (\n              <span className=\"label-text-alt\">Balance: ${formatUSDC(usdcBalance)}</span>\n            )}\n          </label>\n          <input\n            type=\"number\"\n            min=\"0\"\n            step=\"0.01\"\n            placeholder=\"0.00\"\n            className=\"input input-bordered w-full\"\n            value={amount}\n            onChange={e => setAmount(e.target.value)}\n          />\n          <div className=\"flex gap-2 mt-2\">\n            {PRESETS.map(preset => (\n              <button\n                key={preset}\n                type=\"button\"\n                className=\"btn btn-sm btn-outline\"\n                onClick={() => setAmount(preset)}\n              >\n                ${preset}\n              </button>\n            ))}\n          </div>\n        </div>\n\n        <div className=\"form-control\">\n          <label className=\"label\">\n            <span className=\"label-text\">Message (optional)</span>\n            <span className={`label-text-alt ${messageTooLong ? \"text-error\" : \"\"}`}>\n              {message.length}/{MAX_MESSAGE_LENGTH}\n            </span>\n          </label>\n          <textarea\n            className=\"textarea textarea-bordered w-full\"\n            placeholder=\"Say something nice…\"\n            rows={2}\n            value={message}\n            onChange={e => setMessage(e.target.value)}\n          />\n        </div>\n\n        {!connectedAddress ? (\n          <div className=\"flex flex-col items-center gap-2\">\n            <p className=\"text-sm text-center opacity-70\">Connect your wallet to send a tip.</p>\n            <RainbowKitCustomConnectButton />\n          </div>\n        ) : insufficientBalance ? (\n          <button className=\"btn btn-primary\" disabled>\n            Insufficient USDC balance\n          </button>\n        ) : needsApproval ? (\n          <button\n            className=\"btn btn-primary\"\n            onClick={handleApprove}\n            disabled={!hasValidAmount || messageTooLong || isApproving}\n          >\n            {isApproving ? (\n              <>\n                <span className=\"loading loading-spinner loading-sm\" />\n                Approving…\n              </>\n            ) : (\n              \"1. Approve USDC\"\n            )}\n          </button>\n        ) : (\n          <button\n            className=\"btn btn-primary\"\n            onClick={handleTip}\n            disabled={!hasValidAmount || messageTooLong || isTipping}\n          >\n            {isTipping ? (\n              <>\n                <span className=\"loading loading-spinner loading-sm\" />\n                Sending…\n              </>\n            ) : (\n              \"Send tip\"\n            )}\n          </button>\n        )}\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n};\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":859,"cache_read_input_tokens":73429,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":859},"output_tokens":5,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"73d548b6-67a8-4992-addc-781c419ba64d","uuid":"63e0f98e-061a-4b59-9e02-b72380e81fa5","timestamp":"2026-07-24T22:30:23.825Z","request_id":"req_011CdMg1tME8Feo1Rs4JubQ4"}

\"next/image\";\nimport Link from \"next/link\";\nimport { usePathname } from \"next/navigation\";\nimport { hardhat } from \"viem/chains\";\nimport { Bars3Icon, BugAntIcon } from \"@heroicons/react/24/outline\";\nimport { FaucetButton, RainbowKitCustomConnectButton } from \"~~/components/scaffold-eth\";\nimport { useOutsideClick, useTargetNetwork } from \"~~/hooks/scaffold-eth\";\n\n\n\ntype HeaderMenuLink = {\n  label: string;\n  href: string;\n  icon?: React.ReactNode;\n};\n\nexport const menuLinks: HeaderMenuLink[] = [\n  {\n    label: 'Home',\n    href: '/'\n  },\n  {\n    label: 'Debug Contracts',\n    href: '/debug',\n    icon: <BugAntIcon className=\"h-4 w-4\" />\n  }\n];\n\nexport const HeaderMenuLinks = () => {\n  const pathname = usePathname();\n\n  return (\n    <>\n      {menuLinks.map(({ label, href, icon }) => {\n        const isActive = pathname === href;\n        return (\n          <li key={href} className=\"h-full\">\n            <Link\n              href={href}\n              passHref\n              className={`${\n                isActive ? \"bg-base-300\" : \"\"\n              } hover:bg-base-300 focus:!bg-base-300 h-full px-4 text-sm gap-2 flex items-center whitespace-nowrap`}\n            >\n              {icon}\n              <span>{label}</span>\n            </Link>\n          </li>\n        );\n      })}\n    </>\n  );\n};\n\n/**\n * Site header\n */\nexport const Header = () => {\n  const { targetNetwork } = useTargetNetwork();\n  const isLocalNetwork = targetNetwork.id ===
