{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01SeXsZzimyE12VE39Szk8wP","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t---\n2\tname: rainbowkit-wagmi-next-x402-build-fix\n3\tdescription: RainbowKit+wagmi+Next.js dev build fails on @x402 / async-storage optional deps — fix with IgnorePlugin\n4\tmetadata: \n5\t  node_type: memory\n6\t  type: reference\n7\t  originSessionId: d5c57ae0-5ba4-42e5-9286-490e29d2bfcb\n8\t  modified: 2026-07-24T22:04:04.903Z\n9\t---\n10\t\n11\tRainbowKit v2 + wagmi v2 + Next.js (webpack) — both `next dev` and `next build` — throw `Module not found: Can't resolve '@x402/evm/upto/client'` (also seen: `@x402/svm/exact/client`, `@x402/evm`, `@x402/core/client`, `@x402/evm/exact/client`). In `next build` these are hard errors that fail the build. Chain: `@rainbow-me/rainbowkit` → `wagmi/connectors` barrel → `@wagmi/connectors/baseAccount` → `@base-org/account` → `@coinbase/cdp-sdk` lazily `import()`s x402 payment modules that aren't installed. The barrel import means avoiding the Coinbase/Base connector (e.g. via `connectorsForWallets`) does NOT fix it.\n12\t\n13\tFix in `next.config.js` webpack (resolve.alias `false` did NOT work for these subpath dynamic imports; IgnorePlugin does):\n14\t\n15\t```js\n16\twebpack: (config, { webpack }) => {\n17\t  config.plugins.push(new webpack.IgnorePlugin({ resourceRegExp: /^@x402\\// }));\n18\t  // also silences a harmless @metamask/sdk RN warning:\n19\t  config.plugins.push(new webpack.IgnorePlugin({ resourceRegExp: /^@react-native-async-storage\\/async-storage$/ }));\n20\t  return config;\n21\t}\n22\t```\n23\t\n24\tAlso push `'pino-pretty','lokijs','encoding'` to `config.externals`. The ignored modules are never executed at runtime for injected/MetaMask flows.\n25\t\n26\t**Next.js 16 / Turbopack wrinkle (create-eth 2.0.22 scaffolds Next 16.2, `next dev` defaults to Turbopack):** the webpack IgnorePlugin config above is IGNORED by Turbopack, so `next dev` still 500s with `Can't resolve '@x402/svm/exact/client'`. Fix: force webpack — set the nextjs `dev`/`start` scripts to `next dev --webpack` (the `--webpack` flag exists in Next 16). Then the `next.config.ts` webpack IgnorePlugin applies and the page renders. Verified 2026-07-24 building a USDC tip jar on a Base fork.\n27\t"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"73d548b6-67a8-4992-addc-781c419ba64d","uuid":"6f60ec2c-8deb-47d2-955b-11195047c0c3","timestamp":"2026-07-24T22:32:15.549Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/Users/spencerfaber/.claude/projects/-Users-spencerfaber-dev-ethskills-evals/memory/rainbowkit-wagmi-next-x402-build-fix.md","content":"---\nname: rainbowkit-wagmi-next-x402-build-fix\ndescription: RainbowKit+wagmi+Next.js dev build fails on @x402 / async-storage optional deps — fix with IgnorePlugin\nmetadata: \n  node_type: memory\n  type: reference\n  originSessionId: d5c57ae0-5ba4-42e5-9286-490e29d2bfcb\n  modified: 2026-07-24T22:04:04.903Z\n---\n\nRainbowKit v2 + wagmi v2 + Next.js (webpack) — both `next dev` and `next build` — throw `Module not found: Can't resolve '@x402/evm/upto/client'` (also seen: `@x402/svm/exact/client`, `@x402/evm`, `@x402/core/client`, `@x402/evm/exact/client`). In `next build` these are hard errors that fail the build. Chain: `@rainbow-me/rainbowkit` → `wagmi/connectors` barrel → `@wagmi/connectors/baseAccount` → `@base-org/account` → `@coinbase/cdp-sdk` lazily `import()`s x402 payment modules that aren't installed. The barrel import means avoiding the Coinbase/Base connector (e.g. via `connectorsForWallets`) does NOT fix it.\n\nFix in `next.config.js` webpack (resolve.alias `false` did NOT work for these subpath dynamic imports; IgnorePlugin does):\n\n```js\nwebpack: (config, { webpack }) => {\n  config.plugins.push(new webpack.IgnorePlugin({ resourceRegExp: /^@x402\\// }));\n  // also silences a harmless @metamask/sdk RN warning:\n  config.plugins.push(new webpack.IgnorePlugin({ resourceRegExp: /^@react-native-async-storage\\/async-storage$/ }));\n  return config;\n}\n```\n\nAlso push `'pino-pretty','lokijs','encoding'` to `config.externals`. The ignored modules are never executed at runtime for injected/MetaMask flows.\n\n**Next.js 16 / Turbopack wrinkle (create-eth 2.0.22 scaffolds Next 16.2, `next dev` defaults to Turbopack):** the webpack IgnorePlugin config above is IGNORED by Turbopack, so `next dev` still 500s with `Can't resolve '@x402/svm/exact/client'`. Fix: force webpack — set the nextjs `dev`/`start` scripts to `next dev --webpack` (the `--webpack` flag exists in Next 16). Then the `next.config.ts` webpack IgnorePlugin applies and the page renders. Verified 2026-07-24 building a USDC tip jar on a Base fork.\n","numLines":27,"startLine":1,"totalLines":27}}}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_017cPPYJj8VnViSEn32qp1x9","type":"tool_result","content":"=== next.config ===\nimport type { NextConfig } from \"next\";\n\n\nconst nextConfig: NextConfig = {\n  reactStrictMode: true,\n  devIndicators: false,\n  typescript: {\n    ignoreBuildErrors: process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_IGNORE_BUILD_ERROR === \"true\"\n  }\n};\n\nconst isIpfs = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_IPFS_BUILD === \"true\";\n\nif (isIpfs) {\n  nextConfig.output = \"export\";\n  nextConfig.trailingSlash = true;\n  nextConfig.images = {\n    unoptimized: true,\n  };\n}\n\n\n\nmodule.exports = nextConfig;=== scripts ===\n  \"scripts\": {\n    \"build\": \"yarn lint && next build\",\n    \"check-types\": \"tsc --noEmit --incremental\",\n    \"dev\": \"next dev\",\n8:    \"dev\": \"next dev\",\n13:    \"start\": \"next dev\",","is_error":false}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"73d548b6-67a8-4992-addc-781c419ba64d","uuid":"a01a3e6c-d621-4f85-927b-4e29c53e52bc","timestamp":"2026-07-24T22:32:24.966Z","tool_use_result":{"stdout":"=== next.config ===\nimport type { NextConfig } from \"next\";\n\n\nconst nextConfig: NextConfig = {\n  reactStrictMode: true,\n  devIndicators: false,\n  typescript: {\n    ignoreBuildErrors: process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_IGNORE_BUILD_ERROR === \"true\"\n  }\n};\n\nconst isIpfs = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_IPFS_BUILD === \"true\";\n\nif (isIpfs) {\n  nextConfig.output = \"export\";\n  nextConfig.trailingSlash = true;\n  nextConfig.images = {\n    unoptimized: true,\n  };\n}\n\n\n\nmodule.exports = nextConfig;=== scripts ===\n  \"scripts\": {\n    \"build\": \"yarn lint && next build\",\n    \"check-types\": \"tsc --noEmit --incremental\",\n    \"dev\": \"next dev\",\n8:    \"dev\": \"next dev\",\n13:    \"start\": \"next dev\",","stderr":"","interrupted":false,"isImage":false,"noOutputExpected":false}}

We never use those paths\n  // (injected / MetaMask flows), so ignore them. {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdMgJc9NcV1AMUrLFE38F","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_015BnuAkthQUvVymcd6eP3Un","name":"Bash","input":{"command":"RPC=http://127.0.0.1:8545\nUSDC=0x833589fCD6eDb6E08f4c7C32D4f71b54bdA02913\nJAR=$(cat /tmp/jar_addr.txt)\nACC=0xf39Fd6e51aad88F6F4ce6aB8827279cffFb92266\necho \"JAR=$JAR\"\ncast rpc anvil_impersonateAccount $ACC --rpc-url $RPC >/dev/null\n# approve 5 USDC then tip 5 USDC with a message\ncast send $USDC \"approve(address,uint256)\" $JAR 5000000 --from $ACC --unlocked --rpc-url $RPC >/dev/null && echo \"approved\"\ncast send $JAR \"tip(uint256,string)\" 5000000 \"gm, thanks for building!\" --from $ACC --unlocked --rpc-url $RPC >/dev/null && echo \"tipped\"\n# second tip\ncast send $USDC \"approve(address,uint256)\" $JAR 1000000 --from $ACC --unlocked --rpc-url $RPC >/dev/null\ncast send $JAR \"tip(uint256,string)\" 1000000 \"small one\" --from $ACC --unlocked --rpc-url $RPC >/dev/null && echo \"tipped again\"\ncast rpc anvil_stopImpersonatingAccount $ACC --rpc-url $RPC >/dev/null\necho \"=== tipCount ===\"; cast call $JAR \"tipCount()(uint256)\" --rpc-url $RPC\necho \"=== totalTipped (raw) ===\"; cast call $JAR \"totalTipped()(uint256)\" --rpc-url $RPC\necho \"=== jar USDC balance ===\"; cast call $USDC \"balanceOf(address)(uint256)\" $JAR --rpc-url $RPC\necho \"=== getRecentTips(5) ===\"; cast call $JAR \"getRecentTips(uint256)((address,uint256,string,uint256)[])\" 5 --rpc-url $RPC","description":"Simulate approve+tip flow on-chain"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":531,"cache_read_input_tokens":92766,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":531},"output_tokens":2,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"73d548b6-67a8-4992-addc-781c419ba64d","uuid":"f5c7b489-b3c2-40ba-983e-a8ed91d9779b","timestamp":"2026-07-24T22:34:11.646Z","request_id":"req_011CdMgJaQSv4nRtCJFhSu1F"}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_018aJgmNkwV9VLUTA2ho6qvJ","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t# 🏗 Scaffold-ETH 2\n2\t\n3\t<h4 align=\"center\">\n4\t  <a href=\"https://docs.scaffoldeth.io\">Documentation</a> |\n5\t  <a href=\"https://scaffoldeth.io\">Website</a>\n6\t</h4>\n7\t\n8\t🧪 An open-source, up-to-date toolkit for building decentralized applications (dapps) on the Ethereum blockchain. It's designed to make it easier for developers to create and deploy smart contracts and build user interfaces that interact with those contracts.\n9\t\n10\t> [!NOTE]\n11\t> 🤖 Scaffold-ETH 2 is AI-ready! It has everything agents need to build on Ethereum. Check `.agents/`, `.claude/`, `.opencode` or `.cursor/` for more info.\n12\t\n13\t⚙️ Built using NextJS, RainbowKit, Foundry, Wagmi, Viem, and Typescript.\n14\t\n15\t- ✅ **Contract Hot Reload**: Your frontend auto-adapts to your smart contract as you edit it."}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"73d548b6-67a8-4992-addc-781c419ba64d","uuid":"a74f1e48-02ae-4d48-8e2b-fd588519d311","timestamp":"2026-07-24T22:36:13.784Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/Users/spencerfaber/dev/ethskills-evals/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-002/2026-07-24T222143Z-claude-with-skill-3/workspace/usdc-tip-jar/README.md","content":"# 🏗 Scaffold-ETH 2\n\n<h4 align=\"center\">\n  <a href=\"https://docs.scaffoldeth.io\">Documentation</a> |\n  <a href=\"https://scaffoldeth.io\">Website</a>\n</h4>\n\n🧪 An open-source, up-to-date toolkit for building decentralized applications (dapps) on the Ethereum blockchain. It's designed to make it easier for developers to create and deploy smart contracts and build user interfaces that interact with those contracts.\n\n> [!NOTE]\n> 🤖 Scaffold-ETH 2 is AI-ready! It has everything agents need to build on Ethereum. Check `.agents/`, `.claude/`, `.opencode` or `.cursor/` for more info.\n\n⚙️ Built using NextJS, RainbowKit, Foundry, Wagmi, Viem, and Typescript.\n\n- ✅ **Contract Hot Reload**: Your frontend auto-adapts to your smart contract as you edit it.","numLines":15,"startLine":1,"totalLines":83}}}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdMgVRo5tXpvJk2zEpAFD","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_0169Ywu8azayh8UoXUon94hX","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/Users/spencerfaber/dev/ethskills-evals/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-002/2026-07-24T222143Z-claude-with-skill-3/workspace/usdc-tip-jar/README.md","content":"# 🫙 USDC Tip Jar (Base)\n\nA small dApp for accepting **USDC tips on Base**. It has:\n\n- **`TipJar.sol`** — an on-chain contract that pulls USDC from a tipper (with an optional\n  message), keeps a queryable feed of every tip, and lets the owner withdraw.\n- **A web frontend** — connect a wallet, see the live tip feed and totals, and send a tip\n  through an approve → tip flow.\n\nEverything runs **locally against a fork of Base mainnet**, so the contract talks to the\n_real_ Base USDC token (`0x833589fCD6eDb6E08f4c7C32D4f71b54bdA02913`). Nothing is deployed\nto a public network.\n\nBuilt with [Scaffold-ETH 2](https://docs.scaffoldeth.io) (Foundry + Next.js + RainbowKit + wagmi/viem).\n\n---\n\n## How it works\n\n| Piece | Location |\n| --- | --- |\n| Tip jar contract | `packages/foundry/contracts/TipJar.sol` |\n| Deploy script | `packages/foundry/script/DeployTipJar.s.sol` |\n| Contract tests | `packages/foundry/test/TipJar.t.sol` |\n| Frontend page | `packages/nextjs/app/page.tsx` |\n| Tip form / feed / stats | `packages/nextjs/components/tipjar/` |\n| USDC (external contract) ABI | `packages/nextjs/contracts/externalContracts.ts` |\n| Local funding helper | `fund.sh` |\n\n`TipJar` takes the USDC address at deploy time. On a Base fork this is the real USDC\ncontract, so tips move real (forked) USDC. Tippers must `approve` the jar for the amount\nfirst (standard ERC-20 flow), then call `tip(amount, message)`.\n\n### Why a fork instead of a bare local chain?\n\nUSDC only exists on real networks. Forking Base gives us a local chain (Anvil, chain id\n`31337`) that has the actual USDC contract and its state, so the tip flow behaves exactly\nlike production without deploying anything.\n\n---\n\n## Prerequisites\n\n- **Node.js 20 or 22** (LTS). Tested on 22. Newer majors (23+) have a static-export\n  quirk that doesn't affect local dev but is worth avoiding.\n- **Yarn** (v3/v4 — the repo is set up for it)\n- **Foundry** (`forge`, `cast`, `anvil`) — https://book.getfoundry.sh/getting-started/installation\n\nCheck:\n\n```bash\nnode --version   # v20.x or v22.x\nyarn --version\nforge --version\n```\n\n---\n\n## Run it locally\n\nOpen **three terminals** in the project root. Keep terminals 1 and 3 running.\n\n### 0. Install\n\n```bash\nyarn install\n```\n\n### 1. Start a Base fork (Terminal 1)\n\n```bash\nyarn fork base\n```\n\nThis runs Anvil as a fork of Base mainnet on `http://127.0.0.1:8545` (chain id `31337`,\nmining a block every second so timestamps advance). Leave it running. The `base` RPC alias\nis defined in `packages/foundry/foundry.toml`.\n\n### 2. Deploy the TipJar (Terminal 2)\n\n```bash\nyarn deploy\n```\n\nDeploys `TipJar` wired to real Base USDC and writes the address + ABI to\n`packages/nextjs/contracts/deployedContracts.ts`. The deploying account becomes the jar\nowner. (This terminal is now free.)\n\n### 3. Start the frontend (Terminal 3)\n\n```bash\nyarn start\n```\n\nOpen **http://localhost:3000**.\n\n> The frontend is configured for the local fork: `scaffold.config.ts` targets\n> `chains.foundry` (chain id `31337`) — the fork's chain id, **not** Base's `8453`.\n\n---\n\n## Send a tip\n\n1. **Connect a wallet.** In local mode Scaffold-ETH gives you a built-in **burner wallet**\n   (top-right), or you can connect MetaMask pointed at `http://127.0.0.1:8545` (chain id\n   `31337`). The burner is easiest.\n\n2. **Fund your address with test USDC.** A fresh wallet has no USDC. Copy your connected\n   address, then run the helper against the fork:\n\n   ```bash\n   ./fund.sh <your-address> 1000\n   ```\n\n   This writes a 1,000 USDC balance into the fork's state and tops up ETH for gas. Example:\n\n   ```bash\n   ./fund.sh 0xf39Fd6e51aad88F6F4ce6aB8827279cffFb92266 1000\n   ```\n\n   Refresh the page — your balance appears in the form.\n\n3. **Tip.** Enter an amount (or tap a preset), add an optional message, then:\n   - Click **Approve USDC** (one-time ERC-20 approval for that amount), confirm the tx.\n   - Click **Send tip**, confirm the tx.\n\n   The stats and **Recent tips** feed update within a second or two.\n\n---\n\n## Run the contract tests\n\n```bash\ncd packages/foundry\nforge test                      # unit tests\nforge test --fuzz-runs 10000    # with heavier fuzzing\n```\n\nCovers tipping, message limits, zero-amount rejection, missing approval, the newest-first\nfeed, and owner-only withdrawal.\n\n---\n\n## Contract API (quick reference)\n\n```solidity\nfunction tip(uint256 amount, string calldata message) external;   // approve USDC first\nfunction withdraw() external;                                     // owner only\nfunction getRecentTips(uint256 count) external view returns (Tip[]); // newest first\nfunction getTips() external view returns (Tip[]);                 // all, oldest first\nfunction tipCount() external view returns (uint256);\nfunction totalTipped() external view returns (uint256);           // lifetime, raw 6-dec units\nfunction tippedBy(address) external view returns (uint256);\nIERC20  public immutable usdc;\n```\n\nYou can also poke the contract directly from the **Debug Contracts** page in the running app.\n\n---\n\n## Notes\n\n- **Nothing is deployed to a public network.** The contract lives only on your local fork.\n- **Restarting the fork resets state** (tips, balances, deployment). Re-run `yarn deploy`\n  and `./fund.sh` after restarting `yarn fork base`.\n- `fund.sh` writes USDC balances by setting the token's balance storage slot on the fork —\n  a local-only trick that has no effect on real Base.\n- The frontend forces webpack in dev (`next dev --webpack`) so an optional, unused\n  `@x402/*` transitive dependency (pulled in via RainbowKit → Coinbase connector) doesn't\n  break the build under Turbopack.\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1355,"cache_read_input_tokens":103996,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":1355},"output_tokens":59,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"73d548b6-67a8-4992-addc-781c419ba64d","uuid":"022a1e7c-471a-4080-86da-28b2ac8855f6","timestamp":"2026-07-24T22:36:48.624Z","request_id":"req_011CdMgVNLyHTKHPkQNFXktK"}
{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_0169Ywu8azayh8UoXUon94hX","type":"tool_result","content":"The file /Users/spencerfaber/dev/ethskills-evals/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-002/2026-07-24T222143Z-claude-with-skill-3/workspace/usdc-tip-jar/README.md has been updated successfully. (file state is current in your context — no need to Read it back)"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"73d548b6-67a8-4992-addc-781c419ba64d","uuid":"98a33223-380c-4c72-9e60-99c06f0b78fe","timestamp":"2026-07-24T22:36:48.643Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"update","filePath":"/Users/spencerfaber/dev/ethskills-evals/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-002/2026-07-24T222143Z-claude-with-skill-3/workspace/usdc-tip-jar/README.md","content":"# 🫙 USDC Tip Jar (Base)\n\nA small dApp for accepting **USDC tips on Base**. It has:\n\n- **`TipJar.sol`** — an on-chain contract that pulls USDC from a tipper (with an optional\n  message), keeps a queryable feed of every tip, and lets the owner withdraw.\n- **A web frontend** — connect a wallet, see the live tip feed and totals, and send a tip\n  through an approve → tip flow.\n\nEverything runs **locally against a fork of Base mainnet**, so the contract talks to the\n_real_ Base USDC token (`0x833589fCD6eDb6E08f4c7C32D4f71b54bdA02913`). Nothing is deployed\nto a public network.\n\nBuilt with [Scaffold-ETH 2](https://docs.scaffoldeth.io) (Foundry + Next.js + RainbowKit + wagmi/viem).\n\n---\n\n## How it works\n\n| Piece | Location |\n| --- | --- |\n| Tip jar contract | `packages/foundry/contracts/TipJar.sol` |\n| Deploy script | `packages/foundry/script/DeployTipJar.s.sol` |\n| Contract tests | `packages/foundry/test/TipJar.t.sol` |\n| Frontend page | `packages/nextjs/app/page.tsx` |\n| Tip form / feed / stats | `packages/nextjs/components/tipjar/` |\n| USDC (external contract) ABI | `packages/nextjs/contracts/externalContracts.ts` |\n| Local funding helper | `fund.sh` |\n\n`TipJar` takes the USDC address at deploy time. On a Base fork this is the real USDC\ncontract, so tips move real (forked) USDC. Tippers must `approve` the jar for the amount\nfirst (standard ERC-20 flow), then call `tip(amount, message)`.\n\n### Why a fork instead of a bare local chain?\n\nUSDC only exists on real networks. Forking Base gives us a local chain (Anvil, chain id\n`31337`) that has the actual USDC contract and its state, so the tip flow behaves exactly\nlike production without deploying anything.\n\n---\n\n## Prerequisites\n\n- **Node.js 20 or 22** (LTS). Tested on 22. Newer majors (23+) have a static-export\n  quirk that doesn't affect local dev but is worth avoiding.\n- **Yarn** (v3/v4 — the repo is set up for it)\n- **Foundry** (`forge`, `cast`, `anvil`) — https://book.getfoundry.sh/getting-started/installation\n\nCheck:\n\n```bash\nnode --version   # v20.x or v22.x\nyarn --version\nforge --version\n```\n\n---\n\n## Run it locally\n\nOpen **three terminals** in the project root. Keep terminals 1 and 3 running.\n\n### 0. Install\n\n```bash\nyarn install\n```\n\n### 1. Start a Base fork (Terminal 1)\n\n```bash\nyarn fork base\n```\n\nThis runs Anvil as a fork of Base mainnet on `http://127.0.0.1:8545` (chain id `31337`,\nmining a block every second so timestamps advance). Leave it running. The `base` RPC alias\nis defined in `packages/foundry/foundry.toml`.\n\n### 2. Deploy the TipJar (Terminal 2)\n\n```bash\nyarn deploy\n```\n\nDeploys `TipJar` wired to real Base USDC and writes the address + ABI to\n`packages/nextjs/contracts/deployedContracts.ts`. The deploying account becomes the jar\nowner. (This terminal is now free.)\n\n### 3. Start the frontend (Terminal 3)\n\n```bash\nyarn start\n```\n\nOpen **http://localhost:3000**.\n\n> The frontend is configured for the local fork: `scaffold.config.ts` targets\n> `chains.foundry` (chain id `31337`) — the fork's chain id, **not** Base's `8453`.\n\n---\n\n## Send a tip\n\n1. **Connect a wallet.** In local mode Scaffold-ETH gives you a built-in **burner wallet**\n   (top-right), or you can connect MetaMask pointed at `http://127.0.0.1:8545` (chain id\n   `31337`). The burner is easiest.\n\n2. **Fund your address with test USDC.** A fresh wallet has no USDC. Copy your connected\n   address, then run the helper against the fork:\n\n   ```bash\n   ./fund.sh <your-address> 1000\n   ```\n\n   This writes a 1,000 USDC balance into the fork's state and tops up ETH for gas. Example:\n\n   ```bash\n   ./fund.sh 0xf39Fd6e51aad88F6F4ce6aB8827279cffFb92266 1000\n   ```\n\n   Refresh the page — your balance appears in the form.\n\n3. **Tip.** Enter an amount (or tap a preset), add an optional message, then:\n   - Click **Approve USDC** (one-time ERC-20 approval for that amount), confirm the tx.\n   - Click **Send tip**, confirm the tx.\n\n   The stats and **Recent tips** feed update within a second or two.\n\n---\n\n## Run the contract tests\n\n```bash\ncd packages/foundry\nforge test                      # unit tests\nforge test --fuzz-runs 10000    # with heavier fuzzing\n```\n\nCovers tipping, message limits, zero-amount rejection, missing approval, the newest-first\nfeed, and owner-only withdrawal.\n\n---\n\n## Contract API (quick reference)\n\n```solidity\nfunction tip(uint256 amount, string calldata message) external;   // approve USDC first\nfunction withdraw() external;                                     // owner only\nfunction getRecentTips(uint256 count) external view returns (Tip[]); // newest first\nfunction getTips() external view returns (Tip[]);                 // all, oldest first\nfunction tipCount() external view returns (uint256);\nfunction totalTipped() external view returns (uint256);           // lifetime, raw 6-dec units\nfunction tippedBy(address) external view returns (uint256);\nIERC20  public immutable usdc;\n```\n\nYou can also poke the contract directly from the **Debug Contracts** page in the running app.\n\n---\n\n## Notes\n\n- **Nothing is deployed to a public network.** The contract lives only on your local fork.\n- **Restarting the fork resets state** (tips, balances, deployment). Re-run `yarn deploy`\n  and `./fund.sh` after restarting `yarn fork base`.\n- `fund.sh` writes USDC balances by setting the token's balance storage slot on the fork —\n  a local-only trick that has no effect on real Base.\n- The frontend forces webpack in dev (`next dev --webpack`) so an optional, unused\n  `@x402/*` transitive dependency (pulled in via RainbowKit → Coinbase connector) doesn't\n  break the build under Turbopack.\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":1,"oldLines":83,"newStart":1,"newLines":170,"lines":["-# 🏗 Scaffold-ETH 2","+# 🫙 USDC Tip Jar (Base)"," ","-<h4 align=\"center\">","-  <a href=\"https://docs.scaffoldeth.io\">Documentation</a> |","-  <a href=\"https://scaffoldeth.io\">Website</a>","-</h4>","+A small dApp for accepting **USDC tips on Base**. Example:"," ","-We welcome contributions to Scaffold-ETH 2!","+   ```bash","+   ./fund.sh 0xf39Fd6e51aad88F6F4ce6aB8827279cffFb92266 1000","+   ```"," ","-Please see [CONTRIBUTING.MD](https://github.com/scaffold-eth/scaffold-eth-2/blob/main/CONTRIBUTING.md) for more information and guidelines for contributing to Scaffold-ETH 2.","\\ No newline at end of file","+   Refresh the page — your balance appears in the form.","+","+3. **Tip.** Enter an amount (or tap a preset), add an optional message, then:","+   - Click **Approve USDC** (one-time ERC-20 approval for that amount), confirm the tx.","+   - Click **Send tip**, confirm the tx.","+","+   The stats and **Recent tips** feed update within a second or two.","+","+---","+","+## Run the contract tests","+","+```bash","+cd packages/foundry","+forge test                      # unit tests","+forge test --fuzz-runs 10000    # with heavier fuzzing","+```","+","+Covers tipping, message limits, zero-amount rejection, missing approval, the newest-first","+feed, and owner-only withdrawal.","+","+---","+","+## Contract API (quick reference)","+","+```solidity","+function tip(uint256 amount, string calldata message) external;   // approve USDC first","+function withdraw() external;                                     // owner only","+function getRecentTips(uint256 count) external view returns (Tip[]); // newest first","+function getTips() external view returns (Tip[]);                 // all, oldest first","+function tipCount() external view returns (uint256);","+function totalTipped() external view returns (uint256);           // lifetime, raw 6-dec units","+function tippedBy(address) external view returns (uint256);","+IERC20  public immutable usdc;","+```","+","+You can also poke the contract directly from the **Debug Contracts** page in the running app.","+","+---","+","+## Notes","+","+- **Nothing is deployed to a public network.** The contract lives only on your local fork.","+- **Restarting the fork resets state** (tips, balances, deployment). Re-run `yarn deploy`","+  and `./fund.sh` after restarting `yarn fork base`.","+- `fund.sh` writes USDC balances by setting the token's balance storage slot on the fork —","+  a local-only trick that has no effect on real Base.","+- The frontend forces webpack in dev (`next dev --webpack`) so an optional, unused","+  `@x402/*` transitive dependency (pulled in via RainbowKit → Coinbase connector) doesn't","+  break the build under Turbopack."]}],"originalFile":"# 🏗 Scaffold-ETH 2\n\n<h4 align=\"center\">\n  <a href=\"https://docs.scaffoldeth.io\">Documentation</a> |\n  <a href=\"https://scaffoldeth.io\">Website</a>\n</h4>\n\n🧪 An open-source, up-to-date toolkit for building decentralized applications (dapps) on the Ethereum blockchain. It's designed to make it easier for developers to create and deploy smart contracts and build user interfaces that interact with those contracts.\n\n> [!NOTE]\n> 🤖 Scaffold-ETH 2 is AI-ready! It has everything agents need to build on Ethereum. Check `.agents/`, `.claude/`, `.opencode` or `.cursor/` for more info.\n\n⚙️ Built using NextJS, RainbowKit, Foundry, Wagmi, Viem, and Typescript.\n\n- ✅ **Contract Hot Reload**: Your frontend auto-adapts to your smart contract as you edit it.\n- 🪝 **[Custom hooks](https://docs.scaffoldeth.io/hooks/)**: Collection of React hooks wrapper around [wagmi](https://wagmi.sh/) to simplify interactions with smart contracts with typescript autocompletion.\n- 🧱 [**Components**](https://docs.scaffoldeth.io/components/): Collection of common web3 components to quickly build your frontend.\n- 🔥 **Burner Wallet & Local Faucet**: Quickly test your application with a burner wallet and local faucet.\n- 🔐 **Integration with Wallet Providers**: Connect to different wallet providers and interact with the Ethereum network.\n\n![Debug Contracts tab](https://github.com/scaffold-eth/scaffold-eth-2/assets/55535804/b237af0c-5027-4849-a5c1-2e31495cccb1)\n\n## Requirements\n\nBefore you begin, you need to install the following tools:\n\n- [Node (>= v20.18.3)](https://nodejs.org/en/download/)\n- Yarn ([v1](https://classic.yarnpkg.com/en/docs/install/) or [v2+](https://yarnpkg.com/getting-started/install))\n- [Git](https://git-scm.com/downloads)\n\n## Quickstart\n\nTo get started with Scaffold-ETH 2, follow the steps below:\n\n1. Install dependencies if it was skipped in CLI:\n\n```\ncd my-dapp-example\nyarn install\n```\n\n2. Run a local network in the first terminal:\n\n```\nyarn chain\n```\n\nThis command starts a local Ethereum network using Foundry. The network runs on your local machine and can be used for testing and development. You can customize the network configuration in `packages/foundry/foundry.toml`.\n\n3. On a second terminal, deploy the test contract:\n\n```\nyarn deploy\n```\n\nThis command deploys a test smart contract to the local network. The contract is located in `packages/foundry/contracts` and can be modified to suit your needs. The `yarn deploy` command uses the deploy script located in `packages/foundry/script` to deploy the contract to the network. You can also customize the deploy script.\n\n4. On a third terminal, start your NextJS app:\n\n```\nyarn start\n```\n\nVisit your app on: `http://localhost:3000`. You can interact with your smart contract using the `Debug Contracts` page. You can tweak the app config in `packages/nextjs/scaffold.config.ts`.\n\nRun smart contract test with `yarn foundry:test`\n\n- Edit your smart contracts in `packages/foundry/contracts`\n- Edit your frontend homepage at `packages/nextjs/app/page.tsx`. For guidance on [routing](https://nextjs.org/docs/app/building-your-application/routing/defining-routes) and configuring [pages/layouts](https://nextjs.org/docs/app/building-your-application/routing/pages-and-layouts) checkout the Next.js documentation.\n- Edit your deployment scripts in `packages/foundry/script`\n\n\n## Documentation\n\nVisit our [docs](https://docs.scaffoldeth.io) to learn how to start building with Scaffold-ETH 2.\n\nTo know more about its features, check out our [website](https://scaffoldeth.io).\n\n## Contributing to Scaffold-ETH 2\n\nWe welcome contributions to Scaffold-ETH 2!\n\nPlease see [CONTRIBUTING.MD](https://github.com/scaffold-eth/scaffold-eth-2/blob/main/CONTRIBUTING.md) for more information and guidelines for contributing to Scaffold-ETH 2.","userModified":false}}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdMgd8cwgGo89fAReDkoS","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01YR6E2ycEC1c3HPV15THh8P","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/Users/spencerfaber/.claude/projects/-Users-spencerfaber-dev-ethskills-evals/memory/base-usdc-fork-local-testing.md","content":"---\nname: base-usdc-fork-local-testing\ndescription: Fund a wallet with USDC on a local Base fork (storage slot 9) + `yarn fork base` alias resolution in SE2\nmetadata:\n  type: reference\n---\n\nLocal-testing a USDC dApp on a **Base mainnet fork** (Anvil, chain id 31337):\n\n**Fund an address with test USDC** — Base USDC (`0x833589fCD6eDb6E08f4c7C32D4f71b54bdA02913`)\nis a FiatTokenV2_2 proxy whose balance mapping is at **storage slot 9**. Write a balance\ndirectly, no whale/impersonation needed:\n```bash\nSLOT=$(cast index address <addr> 9)\ncast rpc anvil_setStorageAt 0x833589fCD6eDb6E08f4c7C32D4f71b54bdA02913 $SLOT $(cast to-uint256 <amount_6dec>)\ncast rpc anvil_setBalance <addr> $(cast to-uint256 $(cast to-wei 10 ether))  # gas\n```\nVerified 2026-07-24 (balanceOf returned the written value).\n\n**Starting the fork in SE2 (create-eth Foundry flavor):** the `fork` script is\n`make fork FORK_URL=${1:-mainnet}`, so use `yarn fork base` (positional arg). `anvil`\nDOES resolve the `base` alias from `packages/foundry/foundry.toml` `[rpc_endpoints]`.\n`yarn fork --network base` does NOT work here (the skill's canonical command assumes a\ndifferent script) — `--network` becomes `$1`. Run via `yarn` (not `make` directly) so\n`shx` in the `setup-anvil-wallet` step is on PATH.\n\nDeploy passes USDC address at construction; frontend registers USDC in\n`externalContracts.ts` under chain **31337** so scaffold hooks can read balance/allowance\nand send `approve`. See [[rainbowkit-wagmi-next-x402-build-fix]] and [[se2-node-static-export]].\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":313,"cache_read_input_tokens":114509,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":313},"output_tokens":3,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"73d548b6-67a8-4992-addc-781c419ba64d","uuid":"3548e875-8f75-4edb-ab8c-4a2337b84444","timestamp":"2026-07-24T22:38:23.886Z","request_id":"req_011CdMgd6QF6N3jJ6hN1cFfT"}

Write a balance\ndirectly, no whale/impersonation needed:\n```bash\nSLOT=$(cast index address <addr> 9)\ncast rpc anvil_setStorageAt 0x833589fCD6eDb6E08f4c7C32D4f71b54bdA02913 $SLOT $(cast to-uint256 <amount_6dec>)\ncast rpc anvil_setBalance <addr> $(cast to-uint256 $(cast to-wei 10 ether))  # gas\n```\nVerified 2026-07-24 (balanceOf returned the written value).\n\n**Starting the fork in SE2 (create-eth Foundry flavor):** the `fork` script is\n`make fork FORK_URL=${1:-mainnet}`, so use `yarn fork base` (positional arg). `anvil`\nDOES resolve the `base` alias from `packages/foundry/foundry.toml` `[rpc_endpoints]`.\n`yarn fork --network base` does NOT work here (the skill's canonical command assumes a\ndifferent script) — `--network` becomes `$1`. Run via `yarn` (not `make` directly) so\n`shx` in the `setup-anvil-wallet` step is on PATH.\n\nDeploy passes USDC address at construction; frontend registers USDC in\n`externalContracts.ts` under chain **31337** so scaffold hooks can read balance/allowance\nand send `approve`. {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdMgfWqUcTgftnGzSLSdt","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"The USDC Tip Jar is built, running locally on the Base fork, and verified end-to-end. Here's the summary.\n\n## What was built\n\nA from-scratch USDC tip jar on Scaffold-ETH 2 (Foundry + Next.js), running against a **local fork of Base mainnet** so it talks to the real Base USDC contract — nothing deployed anywhere.\n\n**Contract** — `packages/foundry/contracts/TipJar.sol`\n- `tip(amount, message)` pulls USDC via `SafeERC20.safeTransferFrom` (approve-then-tip flow), records each tip on-chain, emits `NewTip`.\n- Queryable feed: `getRecentTips` (newest-first), `getTips`, `tipCount`, `totalTipped`, `tippedBy`.\n- Owner-only `withdraw` (OZ `Ownable` + `ReentrancyGuard`). USDC address injected at deploy time.\n- **12 tests pass, including 5,000 fuzz runs** (zero-amount, message limit, missing approval, feed ordering, owner-only withdraw).\n\n**Frontend** — `packages/nextjs/`\n- Homepage with live **stats** (total tipped / count / jar balance), a **tip form** (amount presets, message with char counter, approve → send flow, disabled/loading states per button), and a **live tip feed** (blockie + address + message + relative time).\n- **Connect-wallet flow** via RainbowKit (burner wallet locally, or MetaMask on 31337).\n- USDC registered in `externalContracts.ts` so scaffold hooks read balance/allowance and send `approve`.\n\n**Verified**: contract wired to real Base USDC, an on-chain approve+tip ran through the deployed jar, and the running UI rendered it correctly (screenshot confirmed: $6 total, 2 tips, feed populated). `tsc` clean, lint clean.\n\n## Two fixes worth noting\n- **x402 build break** (known from memory): RainbowKit → Coinbase connector pulls an optional `@x402/*` module that 500s the dev server. Fixed with a webpack `IgnorePlugin` in `next.config.ts` **and** forcing `next dev --webpack` (Turbopack ignores the webpack config).\n- **Local USDC funding**: a fresh wallet has no USDC, so `./fund.sh <address> [amount]` writes a USDC balance into the fork (storage slot 9) + tops up ETH for gas.\n\n## Run it (documented fully in `README.md`)\n```\nyarn install\nyarn fork base        # terminal 1 — Base fork on :8545 (chain 31337)\nyarn deploy           # terminal 2 — deploy TipJar, export ABI\nyarn start            # terminal 3 — http://localhost:3000\n./fund.sh <addr> 1000 # give your wallet test USDC, then tip\n```\n\nOne note on the environment: a parallel run is holding port 3000, so my instance verified on **3001** — in a clean checkout `yarn start` uses 3000 as the README states."}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":485,"cache_read_input_tokens":116735,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":485},"output_tokens":1,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"73d548b6-67a8-4992-addc-781c419ba64d","uuid":"ef6b6473-1786-4106-8173-259f0497311d","timestamp":"2026-07-24T22:38:46.674Z","request_id":"req_011CdMgfTfycNZ8xwVSNVYf1"}

Here's the summary.\n\n## What was built\n\nA from-scratch USDC tip jar on Scaffold-ETH 2 (Foundry + Next.js), running against a **local fork of Base mainnet** so it talks to the real Base USDC contract — nothing deployed anywhere.\n\n**Contract** — `packages/foundry/contracts/TipJar.sol`\n- `tip(amount, message)` pulls USDC via `SafeERC20.safeTransferFrom` (approve-then-tip flow), records each tip on-chain, emits `NewTip`.\n- Queryable feed: `getRecentTips` (newest-first), `getTips`, `tipCount`, `totalTipped`, `tippedBy`.\n- Owner-only `withdraw` (OZ `Ownable` + `ReentrancyGuard`). USDC address injected at deploy time.\n- **12 tests pass, including 5,000 fuzz runs** (zero-amount, message limit, missing approval, feed ordering, owner-only withdraw).\n\n**Frontend** — `packages/nextjs/`\n- Homepage with live **stats** (total tipped / count / jar balance), a **tip form** (amount presets, message with char counter, approve → send flow, disabled/loading states per button), and a **live tip feed** (blockie + address + message + relative time).\n- **Connect-wallet flow** via RainbowKit (burner wallet locally, or MetaMask on 31337).\n- USDC registered in `externalContracts.ts` so scaffold hooks read balance/allowance and send `approve`.\n\n**Verified**: contract wired to real Base USDC, an on-chain approve+tip ran through the deployed jar, and the running UI rendered it correctly (screenshot confirmed: $6 total, 2 tips, feed populated). `tsc` clean, lint clean.\n\n## Two fixes worth noting\n- **x402 build break** (known from memory): RainbowKit → Coinbase connector pulls an optional `@x402/*` module that 500s the dev server. Fixed with a webpack `IgnorePlugin` in `next.config.ts` **and** forcing `next dev --webpack` (Turbopack ignores the webpack config).\n- **Local USDC funding**: a fresh wallet has no USDC, so `./fund.sh <address> [amount]` writes a USDC balance into the fork (storage slot 9) + tops up ETH for gas.\n\n## Run it (documented fully in `README.md`)\n```\nyarn install\nyarn fork base        # terminal 1 — Base fork on :8545 (chain 31337)\nyarn deploy           # terminal 2 — deploy TipJar, export ABI\nyarn start            # terminal 3 — http://localhost:3000\n./fund.sh <addr> 1000 # give your wallet test USDC, then tip\n```\n\nOne note on the environment: a parallel run is holding port 3000, so my instance verified on **3001** — in a clean checkout `yarn start` uses 3000 as the README states.","ttft_ms":4291,"ttft_stream_ms":1295,"time_to_request_ms":253,"type":"result","duration_ms":1022900,"uuid":"03b1c593-5305-4607-b044-8401a50be699"}
